## Who is where at end of chapter 252 — VOLUME 5 BATCH 0001 DRAFTED (cold reader starts here)

Full record: `state/continuity.md` under ***Established facts, Volume 5 Batch 0001 (ch 239–252) — KEEPING AND BEING KEPT*** and ***Volume 5 running ledger — at end of ch 252***. **Day 506 — the thirteenth of a month. She is past the last working point of a line of work, in somebody's ordinary country, at a stone yard, and she has been booked to the end of the season.**

- **Ning Yan** — **FAR SIDE, past the last working point, at a stone yard and a trestle about six hundred yards past the working ground.** Foundation 9, unchanged for fourteen chapters, **and there is nothing above nine anywhere in the world.** Ceiling on the front of the relicensed licence is still **Foundation the first stage** and **she has still never opened it**; seven conditions referenced and never re-listed; the claim the seventh and last thing on the paper.
- **Body at the last page.** Short breath **four counts everywhere.** **THE RIGHT INDEX FINGER IS GONE, PERMANENTLY** — **four fingers' worth of grip left on that hand**, and it was the hand the load came down across at ch 242. The order gone out of her left hand with the heel of it; eleven holds out of about seventy tried. Every county hour about eleven minutes with the flat of a second finger. Right arm open a third of the time. Aperture cracked through and permanent. **Not hungry since the first Foundation.** Cold the way a stone is cold. Bread from a far-side store at five coppers a day. **Eighteen years old.**
- **What the fourteen chapters did.** **She worked the far reach and the book was opened once** (239). **The country asked nothing and her strength had no price on it, and she got one true answer about the reach from a man with no reason to give it** (240). **The pay-cycle was established — the seventh, the seventeenth, the twenty-seventh — and no day can be moved by anybody** (241). **She took the heavy end and a load came down across the hand with four fingers on it, and there was nobody within nine days of road to tell** (242). **Wei Ran gave a rate and a road, and the road west costs ninety coppers when it opens, and it is shut for the season** (243). **A stranger at a pot gave her three facts about nine days west and she could not ask a second question** (244). **She asked for a season out loud at a trestle on a pay-day** (245). **She worked out that her column is the only instrument on this side of the line and did not stop working** (246). **IRREVERSIBLE: she said her name the second time on that ground and is booked to the end of the season, and cannot be un-entered** (247). **The Hunter said he does not want the claim answered either** (248). **Nine stubs in four marks, laid out clean, and for the first time she can prove she was somewhere** (249). **The fire was free and was not warm, and it was let down without asking her** (250). **The office said out loud, in front of six men, that the claim is actionable on their side** (251). **The price is her column, and she said no once, in her own head, with nobody hearing** (252).
- **The one thing the batch changed.** She is not a woman passing through a place any more. **She is a column of hours in a book that an office four hundred yards away has just been told it can be paid against, and she is the only person in the world who can give that column up, and she has said no once.**
- **The claim.** **Eight hundred jin, standing, entered in good order, travelling on the licence, not affected by the relicensing, and ACTIONABLE ON THE FAR SIDE** — confirmed in the ordinary voice in a room with a stove in it, in front of six men, **by a man who does not know the figure is hers and has never asked.** The price of acting is her own hours. **Not answered, not paid, not assessed, not torn off.** Hou Sanliang unpaid, patient and never met. The date in Lowwater untouched. Luo Ping a direction. **No draft has been written and none has been sent — that is Batch 0002's.**
- **Yuan.** Voice. **Six sentences across fourteen chapters, at most two in one chapter, all present tense, and none of them bought anything.** **No figure of what is left of him is on any page in ch 239–252; the *about six weeks* said once at ch 236 is the last figure of him in the manuscript.** Merit settled **zero** times; nothing killed; nothing cleared; the channel not restored. **Ledger is hers to order and she has not ordered it and has not thought about ordering it.** **The sustained scene in which he is on the page in his own right is planned at ch 272, *The Man the Board Keeps*, in Batch 0003, and nothing in this batch touches it.**
- **Bai Zhu.** **At nine feet at ch 248, in the ordinary voice, with the notebook shut: he does not want the claim answered by the far side either, because an answered claim is an answer somebody above him has to reckon with and he has never believed the reckoning would be the right one — and the same is true of his own order and he has said that before.** The standing one-breath form holds: bought nothing, threatened nothing, touched nothing, entered nothing, asked nothing. **He certified nothing, bought nothing and is not a villain.** He has said he will be on that side of the post for the rest of the run and will not come inside nine feet again unless she puts him there. **His request is still pending and the line's own people still do not know she exists.**
- **Inside the bound, by report only, at ch 244.** **A tower with a light in it that is on at the fourth hour and does not stop. A bundle going down a stair and a clerk's arms going up four flights with it and coming down empty. A floor of nine men and the same nine and all of them still on it.** From a hauler at a pot who has never met Feng Lushi and does not know his name. **Feng does not appear. No scene west of the line. No document arrives. His fair copy of the Foundation column past the line is ch 262 and is unspent.**
- **The far side is ordinary country.** A yard, a trestle, a fire, eleven men and a man at the ramp called Da; a store with a counter and five coppers; a low building with a counter, a stove and six men; a post with a board on it that a keeper of about fifty-five fills off a cart every nine or ten days; a mill two miles east with a wheel that is not turning. **It asked her nothing.** Not one man said her name, and the strongest body within nine days of road is measured on it entirely by what she turns over with a bar.
- **Pay and stubs.** **Pays fall on the seventh, the seventeenth and the twenty-seventh; a day not worked is a day not paid and no day can be moved in either direction by anybody.** Five in, five out, and the number in her hand the same at the start and the end of every day. **Nine counterfoils in four marks in the oilcloth above the Clay Plate, none checkable by anybody within nine days of road, and the dates come out clean.**
- **The register still reads *reconciled*. The Clay Plate is still worn and was not described. MEMBER ABSENT stands three times and was not raised. Shen Xiu unforgiven and unthanked. The three sponsors silent. Ning Wu does not appear. What became of the eleven is not raised. The ch 150 refusal is not softened, deferred, reopened or regretted in any line. The year of non-forgiveness was not mentioned at all in these fourteen chapters and is still carrying.** She does not forgive him and she does not tell him to stop.
- **The road that goes back inside is shut for the season** and gets its opening day in Batch 0002. Wei Ran quoted **ninety coppers** for a person and one bag when it opens; the coming is a different number and nobody has quoted it.
- **The surface answer is NOT paid.** Batch 0001 pays the narrower thing only: *a figure is only paid against something, and the only thing in this country that can be charged for is a day somebody worked.* **The volume's surface answer is ch 267, in a worker's mouth, in short sentences, and Batch 0002 must pay it at the last chapter and not before.**
- **The far-side board is not re-counted.** Its image was ch 236 and ch 238. What is on the page now is the routine: a strip goes up every nine or ten days whether or not there is anything in it.

## Who is where at end of chapter 360 — VOLUME 6 CLOSED AND AUDITED (cold reader starts here)

**After ch 360 — day 848, the twenty-fifth of a month, nine days past the day she reached Nascent Soul 1. She is on flat
ground about a mile and a quarter east of a mill whose wheel has not turned since before she came, in no employment,
with no season, with a gate at one copper a day, a bare board, and a furnace that lights itself.** Full record in
`state/continuity.md` under
***Established facts, Volume 6 Batch 0004 (ch 346–360) — STANDING AND REFUSING TO STAND***, ***Volume 6 running
ledger — at the close of the volume, day 848*** and ***Construction budget — Volume 6 Batch 0004 (ch 346–360),
measured by grep, not promised***.

**THE VOLUME IS CLOSED AND AUDITED.** `outline/volume-06.md` walked line by line against ch 299–360; the table is
**`## Volume 6 close audit — OPENED`** below and the repairs, the findings, the seventeen continuity items and the
protagonist answer are in **`## Volume 6 close audit — CLOSED`** after it. **Sixteen repairs across seventeen chapter
files; seven findings recorded and not repaired; file set after repair 95,110 words of body prose over sixty-two
chapters** (24,529 · 23,975 · 25,056 · 21,550 by batch), **and three more repairs across three of those same files
at the close's own review, recorded in `## Volume 6 close review — REPAIR` below.** Next: **Volume 7 planning**, which writes
`outline/volume-07.md`.

- **Ning Yan — NASCENT SOUL 1, day 845, the twenty-second, reached BY HAND AND NOT BY SOUND. NO BRACKET.** The reach
  begins once, by accident, on a bank past the mill: **a stone she never touched goes the length of her own arm and
  stops and is set down clean, and nobody hears it and nothing is said.** Four men nine feet off do not look up, and
  where he is, in the room with the floor and the table and the two shut doors, nothing at all happens, **because she
  had not put this there — and the surprise does not come back, and it will not come back, and it is not a thing
  anybody gives her twice.** An office writes it down afterward, in a hand she has never seen, because the hand can read
  what happened. **She cannot read the hand.** Brackets 5 and 6 of Volume 6's six were at ch 352 and ch 357 and the
  budget is spent. Paper still Foundation the first stage, never opened.
- **Body:** short breath **four everywhere except a loaded reach, where it is five, permanent, since ch 342**; right
  index gone, four fingers' grip; right arm open half the time, permanent, since ch 288; aperture cracked through and
  permanent; **eight of eleven holds**, since ch 332; turn gone; **the middle-bank method is gone since ch 352 — the
  bar takes two hands she does not have**; not hungry; hands warm; **eighteen.**
- **What the batch changed.** The board at the works **went bare** and the yard goes quiet until the season turns ·
  **Golden Core 8, and with it the heavy end, closed for good** · **the gate down from a third of a reach to a fifth,
  one and a half coppers to one** · **the first twenty-eighth passed with nothing on any road** · **the Hand refused
  once, out loud, and not spent** · **the refusal written on cloth in her own hand and sent back up the road** ·
  **Golden Core 9 and the furnace finished** · **the finding — the second vouching was copied upward out of its own
  office in a hand she has never seen, and she cannot be un-entered twice over** · **the sending refused, out loud, in
  daylight, with her name said, entered in the office's own book with the twenty-sixth against it, and standing** ·
  **a book of hers on a stone under a keeper's ladder at a post** · **and the reach, once.**
- **The three irreversible steps, and none of them is deferred.** The **Hand refused** (ch 355); the **sending
  refused, entered and standing** (ch 358); and the **finding that cannot be un-entered twice over** (ch 357). The two
  stages are on the page at their pinned days.
- **GC8's cost, named once, permanent:** the middle-bank method stops paying — **the bar she learned to carry
  one-handed now needs two hands she does not have, and the heavy end closes to her for good.** Any work measured by
  what comes out of the ground in pieces rather than in length is closed to her, and there is no listing on that board
  she could be put on.
- **GC9's cost, named once, permanent: the finding, not the body.** Nothing came out of her hands or her arm or a hold
  or a count of breath. The furnace of her own, finished. And on the last step of six stages she found out that the
  second vouching was carried **up** out of the office that keeps it and written again in a hand she has never seen.
- **The refusal at ch 358.** *NING YAN, OF LOWWATER. No.* Said in the ordinary voice at the ninth hour of the
  twenty-sixth in a room with a stove and about nine men in it, in front of six of the counter's own, and entered by
  a man behind a counter in the seasonal book — the only book on nine days of road that shuts — as *THE HOLDER IS NOT
  PRESENTED. REFUSED AT THE COUNTER. THE TWENTY-SIXTH.* **He does not write why.** The book now holds three things
  with nothing at the other end of the rule, one of them her refusal, **she put it there herself, and there is no
  hand in that building that can take it out.**
- **Yuan, ch 355 — THE HAND REFUSED ONCE.** The office asks for a second holder under his holding and he will not
  give one. *No.* **Because a second holder is a second person an office can send for**, and it would be on that line
  for as long as the line is there. **And the price of refusing, in his own words and hers, is that he is refusing a
  thing that would make her safer.** Not spent. **He does not buy, advise, explain or advance anything.**
- **Yuan, everywhere else:** three utterances in fourteen chapters outside ch 355 (ch 348, 351, 356), six sentences,
  every one present tense. **Nothing at ch 346, 347, 349, 350, 352, 353, 354, 357, 358, 359 or 360.** Addressed as a
  holder at ch 338 **stands and is not undone**; **being sent for is a thing that can now happen to him in principle,
  and this batch neither resolves it nor has it happen off-page.** The date of the morning he died is not given back
  and ch 272's finding is not softened.
- **The Hunter is on the page, ch 347 only.** Nine feet off a cart track, notebook shut, buys nothing, asks nothing,
  and she asks **him** — for the first time in five months. **His request is still pending. A Wall writ is not issued
  to a field agent. The line's own people still do not know she exists.**
- **The structural answer continues, in four mouths, in four or five sentences each, at ch 346, 350, 353 and 357:**
  the line past the last working point is walked, kept, copied and vouched by offices that never needed a bound, by
  people paid by the day, with families on it, and **nobody east of here has ever needed one thing out of it until she
  asked for her name on a wall.** **The tenth part is not given and nothing proves the crews wrong.**
- **Merit does not settle in this batch. Nothing killed, nothing measured, no figure of what is left of him on any
  page.** `Ledger` returns zero across the batch.
- **Untouched and unraised:** the register still reads *reconciled* and **is not read anywhere in ch 346–360** · the
  Clay Plate worn and **not described** · the relicensed licence **never opened** · MEMBER ABSENT carried and never
  raised · **the year of non-forgiveness carrying and not mentioned once in these fifteen chapters** · Shen Xiu
  unforgiven · *kiln-born* zero · what became of the eleven not raised · **no office named at all and nothing above the
  copying floor entered, described or named** · the surface answer not re-voiced · the seventh condition never listed ·
  the four erased months carried and never named · the claim not reopened · Hou Sanliang paid, never met, not on the
  page · Ning Wu does not appear · **Wei Ran does not appear** · **no second Lung and no second appointment** · Feng
  does not appear · no harem and nothing a romance · **she never says her mother's name**.
- **What is still open and must be still open on the last page of the volume:** the Hunter's request pending ·
  everything above the offices that vouched unread · *kiln-born* in two heads and on no paper · what became of the
  eleven · the register still *reconciled* · the plate still worn · the year still carrying · **and the rooms that
  were refused a presentation, which have now started reading her where she stands instead.**

**After ch 374 — day 899, the sixteenth of the month that began on day 884, fifty days after she reached Nascent Soul
1. She is at a gate and a shed three miles east of a mill whose wheel has not turned since before she came, in no
employment, with no wages, a full board she cannot take a line off, and a furnace that will not light itself.** Full
record in `state/continuity.md` under
***Established facts, Volume 7 Batch 0001 (ch 361–374) — ROOMS BIGGER THAN SHE IS***,
***Volume 7 running ledger — at the last page of Volume 7 Batch 0001, day 899*** and
***Construction budget — Volume 7 Batch 0001 (ch 361–374), measured by grep, not promised*** and
***Volume 7 Batch 0001 — REVIEW REPAIR***.

**VOLUME 7 BATCH 0001 IS ON THE PAGE AND REPAIRED — ch 361–374, fourteen chapters, 27,114 words of body prose, average ~1,937,
day 849 to day 899.** Drafted against `outline/volume-07.md` and against
`workspace/volume-07-batch-0001/PROMPT.md`, which is that batch's card. **The prompt's corrected handoff was right and
the page followed it: the batch opens on day 849, the twenty-sixth, the day after ch 360 closed — not on the stage
day and not on the day the strip came.** **Reviewed after the batch was saved and repaired on the page: the money at ch 374 did not add up
and now reads thirteen in, one trip west at eight, five in the fold, with the sum said out loud; every chapter was
brought inside the 1,800–2,700 guide (23,101 → 27,114); `about` came down from 312 to 197; ten recycled paragraph
openings were recast; five of the six *What* titles were renamed; and ch 374's re-voicing of ch 371's cost line — a

- **Ning Yan — NASCENT SOUL 2, day 886, the third of a month, taken IN THE OPEN AT THE SEVENTH HOUR WITH NINE MEN ON
  A BANK ABOUT SIXTY FEET LONG AND TWO OF THEM NINE FEET OFF HER.** The bracket at ch 371 is
  **bracket 1 of Volume 7's six in sixty** and matches the far-side house form character for character. **The next
  five brackets are at ch 383, 394, 404, 412 and 420 and nowhere else in the volume.**
  **Cost, named once, permanent: the furnace will not light itself and she cannot light it alone.** She tries it nine
  times over three hours and it comes up about a finger's width and stops every time. **A stage cannot be built by one
  pair of hands**, and there is no person inside nine days of road she is entitled to stand in a shed with and try.
- **Body:** short breath **four everywhere except a loaded reach, where it is five**, permanent, since ch 342; right
  index gone, four fingers' grip; right arm open half the time, permanent, since ch 288; aperture cracked through and
  permanent; **eight of eleven holds**; turn gone; **the middle-bank method gone since ch 352 — the bar takes two
  hands**; not hungry; **HANDS COLD — they had been warm for four months, warm the way a furnace is warm, and they went
  cold within a moment of the bar stopping on the bank at the third hour of the second of a month**; **eighteen.**
- **What the batch changed.** The reading **left the room** — a strip went east on the end-of-month cart and **did not
  come back on the cart that takes the empty trays up**, and nobody on that ground has ever sent anything east ·
  **the season turned on the eleventh** and the board came back full of eleven dressed-stone lines her right hand
  cannot take · **the work narrowed to reading and nobody on that ground will pay for reading** · **the yard of nine
  men at the turn, where she is the ninth and the smallest thing in it and a man with a rule in his hand priced her
  body in about two seconds and was right about both the thing she could do and the thing she could not** ·
  **a length three days in seven at that yard, unpaid, paid in a cup of something hot from a woman who has never asked
  her name** · **a bar that went across her on a bank with nine men on it, stopped by the furnace in her own shed**
  · **the fire gone out and thirteen days of a ring that comes up a finger's width and stops** · **a column nine days
  of road east with two lines at the foot of it where it had four for four months** · **the Hunter, once, nine feet
  off, and she asked him what a room on this ground is for** · **merit settled once on a listed hedge clearing, six
  days and four hours told out loud twice and spent out of her foundation on nothing** · **the first stage she has
  ever taken that people on that ground watched her take.**
- **The batch's irreversible step, and it is this batch's own:** **ch 371 — the first stage taken in the open with
  people watching.** Two men on a bank at the seventh hour have seen a thing happen to her. **From ch 371 every stage
  she takes is a public fact on that ground and no card in Batch 0002 may put that back the way it was.** The
  volume's break (ch 402) and the volume's refusal (ch 417) are both untouched and are not pre-empted, planned or
  rehearsed anywhere in these fourteen chapters. **She refuses nothing in this batch.**
- **NS 2's cost in one plain sentence about one person:** she is eighteen years old and the only thing on nine days of
  road that ever came to her without being asked has stopped coming, and the only way to get it back is to put a person
  in a room with her, and there is nobody she is entitled to ask.
- **Yuan, across the batch:** nine utterances in fourteen chapters outside the second hour of ch 370, plus the share
  in ch 370 told twice in the present tense. **Every one of them buys nothing and advises nothing.** He knows at ch 362
  that she has counted a month three times and counted one of them wrong. **No Hand is spent and no figure of what is
  left of him is on any page.** `Ledger` returns **zero** across all fourteen files. **Being sent for is not opened,
  not foreshadowed in his mouth and does not happen to anybody else.** No frame scene.
- **The Hunter is on the page, ch 369 only.** Nine feet off the cart track, notebook shut, buys nothing, asks nothing,
  and she asks **him** — *what is a room on this ground for* — and he answers in the ordinary voice and at no cost to
  either of them: **a room is where a thing is put so that four people can agree about it later.** **His request is
  still pending. A writ is not issued to a field agent. The line's own people still do not know she exists.**
- **The structural answer continues, in one mouth of Volume 7's six, at ch 365**, in a carter who drives the long
  road east and has been turned back at the end of it four hundred times, and turning back is what people pay for.
  **The tenth part is not given and nothing proves the crews wrong.**
- **Untouched and unraised:** the register still reads *reconciled* and **nothing in this batch reads it** · the Clay
  Plate worn and **not described** · the relicensed licence **never opened** and never referred to · MEMBER ABSENT
  carried and never raised · **the year of non-forgiveness carrying and not mentioned once in these fourteen
  chapters** · Shen Xiu unforgiven · *kiln-born* zero · what became of the eleven not raised · the four erased months
  never named · the ch 150 refusal not softened · **no office named at all and nothing above the copying floor
  entered, described or named** · the surface answer not re-voiced · the claim not reopened · Hou Sanliang paid, never
  met, not on the page · **Ning Wu and Ning Bo not on the page** · **Wei Ran does not appear** · Feng does not appear
  and no second Lung and no second appointment · no harem and nothing a romance · **she never says her mother's
  name** · **nothing in this batch kills anybody** · **not one word of the Order is on the page** · `Ledger` zero.
- **What is still open and must be still open on the last page of this batch:** the Hunter's request pending ·
  everything above the offices that vouched unread and unnamed · the fine hand and what it says about her ·
  *kiln-born* in two heads and on no paper · what became of the eleven · the register still *reconciled* · the plate
  still worn · the year still carrying · **and the strip that went east on the end-of-month cart and did not come

- **Three new people were used in this batch and none of them is one of Volume 7's three named ones.** **Rao, Dai and

- **Ning Yan — NASCENT SOUL 3, day 924, the eleventh of the month beginning 914, at the seventh hour on the third floor
  of the counting house, in a room with the door shut and nothing else alive in it.** The bracket at ch 383 is **bracket
  2 of Volume 7's six in sixty** and matches the far-side house form character for character. **The next four brackets
  are at ch 394, 404, 412 and 420 and nowhere else in the volume.**
  **Cost, named once, permanent: her hand stops being able to lie.** A line she puts in ink in her own hand is a thing
  she means; she can still say a false thing out loud; **she can no longer enter a false line in any book on that
  ground and there is no hand she can pay to enter one for her.** Not a limb, not a hold, not a count of breath.
- **Body:** short breath **four everywhere except a loaded reach, where it is five**, permanent, since ch 342; right
  index gone, four fingers' grip; **right arm open half the time, permanent, since ch 288, and it cannot be closed into
  a fist at all** since a four-foot ditch came in on it at the fifth hour on the twenty-first of the month beginning 914
  and the arm went into the other thing for two seconds; aperture cracked through and permanent; **eight of eleven
  holds**; turn gone; **the middle-bank method gone since ch 352 — the bar takes two hands**; not hungry; **HANDS COLD
  on every road, and warm in one room on nine days of road when a woman of forty-five is in it**; **eighteen.**
- **What the batch changed.** **A furnace that is not a place** — it comes up in whatever room she is standing in if
  there is another living thing in that room, it came up under a man's hand on the floor of a low building and took the
  skin off two inches of his palm and a hand's width of his forearm, **she said *yes* out loud when he asked her and did
  not say the four words she had** · **the counting house, the copying floor, and the third floor above them — nine
  steps, a landing with a turn in it, forty books open at once, and the largest set of open books she has stood in front
  of** · **Rao, forty-five, four feet ten, a thumb gone off at the first joint, sixteen years, forty thousand columns,
  who has told her in eleven words that there is no line on that floor for her and is right** · **a man with a rule in
  his hand who asked her for the first time in seven months, a day early, and said he was telling her now so it would
  not be a thing that happened to her tomorrow** · **a listed ditch line, four hundred yards by the length, four feet,
  off a works board in two days and rubbed off his own board with his own thumb, with no name of hers in that yard** ·
  **the first offer of a line entered for her by somebody else in the ordinary hand for a fee, at ten coppers, which she
  can neither pay nor take and does not refuse** · **a false line that can no longer get into any book on that ground,
  and nobody on that ground knows it** · **and at ch 388 she said a woman's name out loud in a room, asked her for four
  hours a month, and gave her own name to get it.**
- **The batch's irreversible step, and it is this batch's own: ch 388.** **“Rao.”** — the first name she has said
  out loud on that ground in seven months — **and then “Ning Yan. Of Lowwater,” written by hand in a stair book that
  goes up the mill road on the first at the sixth hour with a cord round the tray and is copied out on the ninth by a
  man who cannot read the fine hand and has never been able to.** Rao said yes in eleven words and ruled out being paid,
  thanked or honoured before it happened. **She cannot read it, nobody inside nine days of road can read it back to her,
  and there is no hand in that building that can take it out.** **She asked for it out loud and she got it and it is the
  first thing she has ever put into a room that she cannot get out of again.** **Batch 0002's second irreversible
  moment is at ch 378**, where she let a man's hand burn rather than say four words in a room with nine men in it.

  **Cost, named once, permanent: she cannot be given anything.** A gift enters the column and the column can be
  read. **She can still be owed. She can still refuse to pay.** Paid on the page at ch 404 with a cup of something hot
  that went cold on a bench.
- **Body:** short breath **four everywhere except a loaded reach, where it is five**, permanent, since ch 342; right
  index gone, four fingers' grip; **right arm open half the time, permanent, since ch 288, and it cannot be closed into
  a fist at all**; aperture cracked through and permanent; **eight of eleven holds**; turn gone; **the middle-bank
  method gone since ch 352 — the bar takes two hands**; not hungry; **HANDS COLD on every road, and warm in one room
  when a woman is in it**; **eighteen.**
- **What the batch changed.** **The volume's midpoint, at ch 390: Rao read her column back to her, all of it, in the
  ordinary voice, in a room, and the column contains a refusal and it is the only refusal in the set — and the
  strongest thing in that building is a column, said once, in Rao's mouth, and not said again in the volume** ·
  **Rao's structural answer, mouth three of six** · **a listed sill course at the south end of the yard at the turn,
  by the weight, thirty-one dressed stones, off the board with the twenty-eighth against it** · **Nascent Soul 4 at
  the seventh hour with nine men on a yard, and the reach put into a stone on purpose at the eighth hour with a man's
  leg under it, and the stone went the length of her own arm sideways into a hole** · **nine deliberate uses in nine
  days and five dressed stones out of the low side of a yard, one of them put there trying to undo an earlier one** ·
  **merit settled once on the listed line, four days and two hours, told out loud twice before it was spent, spent on
  nothing, and the price is four days of a fire that did not come up** · **the tray going up the mill road on the
  first at the sixth hour with a cord round it and her name in it** · **the man with a brush and the ninth, and her
  name in two books** · **a woman who has driven clay for eleven years, mouth four of six** · **a yard book that has
  come back down the road twice asking for a holder** · **a strip that came into a counting house with no cart on the
  mill road** · **the third frame scene at ch 401, and the break at ch 402** · **and at ch 404 a cup of something hot
  put into her hands for the thirteenth time in seven weeks that her hand would not take.**
- **The batch's irreversible step, and all three costs were named before they were spent: ch 402 — the answer goes
  up, and she can never again refuse a reading.** A man behind a counter read her a question off a strip in daylight
  on a step and she said *Yes*, in the ordinary voice, at four feet, and he wrote eleven words in the seasonal book:
  *Ning Yan, of Lowwater. At the gate three miles east of the mill. The twenty-second.* **Not because there is a rule
  against it. Because an office on this ground now holds an answer that has already been given, and a second refusal
  would be a refusal of an answer already given, and an office that holds an answer already given does not accept a
  second one.** **Nothing takes the refusal at ch 358 out of its book; the man behind the counter said so on the step
  and is right. What has gone is the second time, not the entry.**
- **The third frame scene, ch 401, days 993–994, away from her hearing, and it is the book's protagonist's act.**
  Not the field of ch 272 and not the room with the two shut doors of ch 338. **A man who is held lets a send reach
  him and answers it, and the answering is the break: he turns his attention toward it.** Every sentence he speaks
  is in the present tense. **Being answerable is a third thing and it is worse than being sendable, because an answer
  can be quoted** — a finding can be denied, a sending can be refused once, and an answer cannot be refused at all.
  **It goes out of the ground under her feet, not out of her hands. His unentered state, his unnamed state and the
  date of the morning he died all stay gone and none of them is given back. He does not do it twice.**
- **Yuan, across the batch:** he speaks in five chapters and eleven quoted paragraphs — ch 393 ×2, ch 394 ×1, ch 396
  ×3, ch 400 ×2, ch 401 ×3 — **every sentence present tense, buying nothing, advising nothing and advancing nothing.**
  He appears in his own frame nowhere else in ch 390–404. **No figure of what is left of him is on any page.** `Ledger`
  returns **zero** across all fifteen files. **Being answerable as of ch 401 and it is a third thing.** *(The earlier
  version of this line said *five passages*, which is true of the five chapters and false of the paragraphs, and the
  review read it against ch 401 and counted three quoted blocks there, which is what ch 401 has. Both numbers now stand
  with the chapters named.)*
- **Untouched and unraised:** the register still reads *reconciled* and **nothing in this batch reads it** · the Clay
  Plate worn and **not described** · the relicensed licence **never opened** and never referred to · MEMBER ABSENT
  carried and never raised · **the year of non-forgiveness carrying and not mentioned once in these fifteen chapters** ·
  Shen Xiu unforgiven · *kiln-born* zero · what became of the eleven not raised · the four erased months never named ·
  the ch 150 refusal not softened · **no office named at all, in any mouth, and nothing above the third floor entered,
  described or named** · the surface answer not re-voiced · **the structural answer in two mouths of Volume 7's six, at
  ch 391 and ch 398; the tenth part is not given and nothing proves the crews wrong** · the claim not reopened · Hou
  Sanliang paid, never met, not on the page · **Ning Wu and Ning Bo not on the page** · **Wei Ran does not appear** ·
  Feng does not appear and no second Lung and no second appointment · no harem and nothing a romance · **she never
  says her mother's name** · **not one word of the Order is on the page** · **the Hunter is not on the page** and his
  request is still pending and the people who live on that line still do not know she exists · **the fine hand was
  not read and the one person whose trade it is is Batch 0004's** · `Ledger` zero · **nothing in this batch kills
  anybody.**
- **What is still open and must be still open on the last page of this batch:** the Hunter's request pending ·
  everything above the third floor, and above the counting house, and above the standing room two miles up the mill
  road, unread and unnamed · **the fine hand, and the one person whose trade it is** · *kiln-born* in two heads and
  on no paper · what became of the eleven · the register still *reconciled* · the plate still worn · the year still
  carrying · **and the strip that went east on the end-of-month cart and did not come back, and the column that came
  back with two lines off it.**
- **Six threads Batch 0003 opened that Batch 0004 must not lose:** **the second line in the seasonal book and the
  standing refusal of every reading after it** · **the reach that goes four feet and does not go back, and five stones
  in the low side of a yard** · **a page going up the mill road with two holders required against a date on it** ·
  **her name in two books that shut, asked out loud to be taken out and not taken out** · **the cup she cannot take
  from a woman in an office end who has never asked her name** · **and an answer on that ground that can be quoted by
  somebody neither of them has ever met.**
- **Rao, Dai and Geng: Rao is now on the page at ch 390, 391, 394, 395, 396, 398, 400 and 403 and is the volume's
  midpoint and its second structural mouth. Dai and Geng are both still unspent and are Batch 0004's.** The keeper
  with the paste pot is not on the page in this batch and was not asked for anything. **She did not go to the post, she
  did not touch the book on the stone, and she is not going to ask him.**

## Volume 8 Batch 0004 — REVIEW REPAIR

**The review of `volume-08-batch-0004` returned nine findings and all nine are closed. Three were must-fix and two of those three were internal contradictions on the page that had already propagated into the state files. No chapter was restarted, no beat, date anchor, coin figure, character mouth, spend, stop, bracket or stage moved, and nothing in the planned plot changed.** Thirty-one sentences across six of the fourteen files; the batch is 65 words shorter than the drafting phase left it. **Full record at `state/continuity.md` §*Volume 8 Batch 0004 — external review, and the repair pass that answered it*.**

**The three that mattered, and what a cold reader would otherwise have inherited:**

- **ch 478 contradicted itself on how many times she has said his name**, and the volume's last scene is built on the number — one line said eleven answered and about thirty unanswered in seven years, the closing beat said eleven in seven years, four answered, this the twelfth. **It is now eleven occasions in seven years, four answered, stated once, and ch 478 is the twelfth.**
- **ch 474 counted the refusal as both the third time and the fourth entry.** The two were never in conflict — three refusals, four pages in books, because the entry at seventeen was not a refusal — and the chapter said both in one scene while `state/current.md`, `state/continuity.md` and `state/chapter-summaries.md` had inherited the fourth and `state/current.md`'s quotation of Xie Changyin had inherited the third. **Xie Changyin says four now, the paragraph that lists the three prior pages says four, and the state files are correct rather than contradicted.**
- **ch 470 carried a bare volume citation — `since Volume 4`** — which falsifies a recorded zero, because the pattern the budget row prints cannot match a numeral after the word. This manuscript has caught itself twice already. **Recast to ground as *since she was fourteen*, and authorial scaffolding across all fifty-eight chapters of Volume 8 is 0 on the widened pattern, re-measured after the repair.**

**The four that should have been fixed:** the retired hedged-duration construction back in recast form (*about ten seconds*, *about four minutes*) — both plain counts now, and the budget row states the nine that still stand rather than claiming the category is clean · **ch 474's four restatements of *there is no hand that takes it out*, now two** · **an unexplained byte-identical duplicate paragraph at ch 470 and ch 476, broken** · and **`eleven` at eleven times in ch 478 and thirteen in ch 465, now 9 and 11**, the arithmetic-bearing instances kept.

**The two on process:** **the volume-close phase was missing**, and **`state/current.md`'s *Next phase* bullet had the live prompt buried under two thousand characters of superseded prompt.** The Batch 0004 drafting phase wrote `workspace/volume-09-planning/PROMPT.md` where a close prompt belonged — it complied with the prompt it was given, and the defect is upstream in `workspace/volume-08-batch-0004/PROMPT.md`. **`workspace/volume-09-planning/` is removed and `workspace/volume-08-close/PROMPT.md` is the one next phase.** The cost was not tidiness: with no close phase, Volume 8's fifty-eight-chapter figures were self-certified by the phase that wrote fourteen of the fifty-eight chapters, and the three-batch word-count discrepancy had no phase left to settle it.

**Two more defects the review did not find, found while repairing the three above:** **ch 470 dated the form-reading on the twelfth of a month five days after the eighteenth, where the outline's day table and ch 473 both put it on the first of the month beginning 1,304** · and **ch 476 said she had been at the top of a realm for eleven days, where the stage is at day 1,291 and ch 476's fourteenth is at day 1,317, so it is twenty-six.**

**What the repair settled that had been standing open.** The Batch 0004 phase recorded that its re-measure reproduced Batches 0001–0003 higher than their own tables by 668, 188 and 462 words and declined to reconcile it. **It is reconciled: the gap is exactly the `##` section-heading word count in each of those three batches, because their tables were measured with headings excluded and the volume re-measure counted them.** No batch figure was ever wrong and no chapter before ch 465 was opened. **Batch 0004 is 29,125 and Volume 8 is 107,669 on the convention the tables use; 28,620 and 105,846 on the other; both are now recorded with both methods.**

**Also measured and left for the close:** **twenty-nine chapter files in Volume 8 have no trailing newline** — Batch 0001's fourteen and Batch 0003's fifteen. The Volume 7 close fixed 250 such files. It is not a prose defect and it does not touch a word count. **It is `workspace/volume-08-close/PROMPT.md`'s.**

**What the repair did not do: audit Volume 8.** That is what the close phase is for, and Batches 0001, 0002 and 0003 have still never been audited at volume level.

### The findings. Twenty-two, in six classes. None is repaired here. **(F22 was added by the close review.)**

**A. The calendar — one defect, twenty-five instances, ten chapters.**

- **F1 — Batch 0004's relative-month figures do not reproduce against the volume's own calendar.** The volume counts
  elapsed months as month-begins crossed, and the months in Volume 8 begin on days 1,064 · 1,094 · 1,124 · 1,154 · 1,184
  · 1,214 · 1,244 · 1,274 · 1,304 · 1,334. **Seven instances on the page, in six places, get that exactly right and are
  the evidence the convention is the drafters' own**: ch 462 ×2, ch 465, ch 472 ×2, ch 478's *two months back*, and
  ch 478's *eighty-four days*. **Twenty-five instances across ten chapters fail — ch 467, 469, 470, 471, 472, 473, 474,
  476, 477 and 478, and no other chapter.** The worst is the one the volume's late register rests
  on: **the ch 417 refusal is called *four months back* eight times across ch 469, 472, 473 and 474, and ch 417 is the
  nineteenth of the month beginning 1,034 — day 1,052 — which from ch 474's day 1,306 is nine months, not four.**
  *The twenty-five, measured:* ch 469 ×3, ch 472 ×2, ch 473, ch 474 ×2 on ch 417 (nine, not four) · ch 474 on the ch 449
  silence (four, not five) · ch 473, ch 474 and ch 477 ×2 on the shed at a mill (seven, not five) · ch 471 and ch 474 on the
  woman of twenty-two at her bench (three and four, not five) · ch 470 on the ch 434 no-take (five, not four) · ch 470 on
  the ch 432 date in a doorway (five, not four) · ch 467 on the sixth of a month (six, not four) · ch 470, ch 476 ×2 and
  ch 478 on the last loaf of bread (three, four and five, not two) · ch 478 on the ch 462 stop (three, not two) · ch 477
  on the ch 466 board (two, not four) · ch 471 on the ch 419 refusal (seven, not eight).
- **F2 — ch 474 lists the three refusals out of order, with two of three intervals wrong and the two errors pulling in
  opposite directions.** *Four months ago in a room with a lamp, to a man of forty-three… Five months ago in daylight on
  a road, to a woman of sixty-one, in silence. And now…* and then *Three refusals in eight years*. **A five-months-back
  event cannot precede a four-months-back one in a chronological list; neither figure is right.** The chapter's substance
  stands — three refusals, the third of them hers, the fourth page in a book, the first one she chose.
- **F3 — ch 478 carries ch 465's interval forward eighty-one days without re-deriving it.** *No bread since the
  twenty-eighth of a month two months back* is exactly right at ch 465 and appears again at ch 470, twice at ch 476 and
  at ch 478, where it is five months. **The second time in this run that a carried figure has falsified a recorded zero,
  and the first time it has falsified itself inside one volume.**
- **THE REPAIR IS TEN CHAPTER FILES AND TWENTY-SIX LINES. IT IS A REPAIR PHASE'S AND NOT THIS ONE'S. The absolute days
  are all right. The intervals are not.** *(Corrected by the close review: this line previously said seventeen files. Seven
  of them — ch 421, 425, 432, 434, 445, 449 and 466 — are **referents** in the table above, the days the wrong figures are
  measured against, and no wrong figure stands on those seven pages.)*

**B. Card vocabulary on the page — one defect, three headings.**

- **F4 — three section headings are outline instructions**, in the exact class the Volume 7 close recorded as F3 and left
  unrepaired: `## His Mouth, In Five Pieces` at ch 457, the **byte-identical** `## His Mouth, In Five Pieces` at ch 461,
  and `## The Sixth Mouth` at ch 477. *Mouth* is `outline/volume-08.md`'s own term for a structural-answer beat and *in
  five pieces* is the card's phrasing for *four or five short sentences each*; *The Sixth Mouth* is the label on the
  outline's own six-mouths table row. Volume 7's pages carry `## Mouth Five` (ch 409) and `## Mouth Six` (ch 415) and
  the Volume 7 close named this class as the one the Batch 0003 review had already repaired at ch 401 and ch 404. **The
  class recurred in Volume 8 and reached Batch 0004, which passed review twice. The repair is three headings, not three
  chapters.**

**C. Recorded figures that do not reproduce — six.**

- **F5 — the Batch 0004 `about…` row** records **136** and states the method as case-insensitive. Measured `\babout\b`,
  whole word, case-insensitive, body prose, headings excluded: **138**. **136 is the case-sensitive count.** Volume-wide
  **470**, by batch **196 · 61 · 75 · 138**, average 8.1 a chapter against Volume 7's 10.1.
- **F6 — the bare capital `Order` rows** record **7 across the volume and 3 in Batch 0004**. Measured, case-sensitive,

  returns **27** and every one is the common noun. **The Order's name is spoken once in fifty-eight chapters.**
- **F7 — the retired seven phrases.** Four blocks record **0 as phrases**. On the seven exact strings: *nobody asked* **2**
  (ch 436, ch 442) · *nobody was thanked* **0** · *there is no form* **0** · *there is no column* **0** · *the record
  remained* **0** · *nobody knew* **1** (ch 439) · *nobody said anything* **0**. **Widened to the flex family the

  form* 2 (ch 445, ch 453) · *There is no second column* 1 (ch 431) — the family stands at 10.**

**D. Recorded and not repaired, with the reason — nine bullets, F11 through F21.**

- **F11 — the `eighty-four` density.** Measured case-insensitive, whole-word, raw files, **including the ordinal
  `eighty-fourth`: 18 volume-wide** — ch 464 ×12, ch 466, ch 468, ch 470 ×2, ch 478 ×2. Measured case-sensitive lowercase
  and the bare number only: **13, of which 7 are in ch 464.** **Both are exact and the method is in the sentence with
  each; the close published the first without the second and the review caught it.** The Batch 0004 review reduced
  `eleven` in ch 465 and ch 478 as a tic and did not reduce this, and **ch 464 doubles it inside one sentence.** The
  density is a drafter's habit and belongs to a volume-scale pass; the three-to-five-instance repair at ch 464 holds on
  either count, because ch 464 is the hotspot on both.
- **F12 — `erased` is 1, at ch 424**: *The four erased months are the only thing she has ever thrown away and she did not
  do that on purpose.* Volume 7 measured `erased` at 0 as its evidence for fact 5. **In Volume 8 the four erased months
  are referred to once, in narration, in her own reasoning about never throwing a line away. No month is named, no date
  given, nobody named, the silence unbroken, nothing forgiven or softened.** The fact holds; the measured zero does not
  carry forward and is not inherited as one.
- **F13 — `Bai Zhu` is 1, at ch 440.** `Hunter` and `Bai Zhu` both returned 0 across Volumes 6 and 7. **The man with the
  notebook is named on the page for the first time in the manuscript, in her mouth, at the second of his two Volume 8
  appearances, and she asks him her question in the same scene.** A change, not a breach; Volume 9 inherits a reader who
  knows his name.
- **F14 — `plate` is 1, at ch 441**, named once and described in no chapter.
- **F15 — `licence` is 2, at ch 425 and ch 449**, both in Xie Changyin's mouth and both the Order's own paper;
  `license` is 0. **The relicensed licence against her ribs is never called anything and never opened.**
- **F16 — twenty-nine chapter files have no trailing newline**: Batch 0001's fourteen and Batch 0003's fifteen. **Twenty-
  nine bytes, for a repair phase.** It does not touch a word count and it is not a prose defect.
- **F17 — `about…`, 470 volume-wide**, and **F18 — `whole of`, 83 volume-wide**, and **F19 — the *nobody* family at
  221**, and **F20 — `eleven` at 229**, densest at ch 452 with 17. **These four are volume-scale signatures and a
  volume-scale pass is what they belong to.** The *nobody* family at 3.8 a chapter against Volume 7's 0.6 is the most
  exposed of the four, and **the outline's own reason holds and is recorded: Volume 8 is the first volume of the run with a
  large number of people in it who do not know her.** Not a licence for it, and not swept here.
- **F21 — eleven duplicated paragraph texts at 22 instances**: two planted and recorded (the bill at ch 466/467, the
  anonymity line at ch 475/478), one carried beat (*He is not gone*, ch 463/478), **one a verbatim restatement of the
  ch 434 cost — *She can still say no. She just cannot make the no take.* — byte-identical at ch 460 and ch 470** — and
  seven furniture.

**F. Section-heading identity — one defect, one chapter. (F22, added by the close review.)**

- **F22 — `chapters/volume-08/chapter-0451.md` lines 101 and 127 are both `## The Lane End`, so one chapter shows the
  same section name twice in a contents list.** *(Added by the close review; this is the finding the close could not
  have caught.)* **The reason is the method, not the care: the duplicate scan ran on body-prose blocks at six words or more,
  and `##` lines were outside it by method.** Re-run over every block, headings included, the volume gives **15 duplicated
  texts at 30 instances**, and four of those texts are `##` heading pairs — ch 421/428, 436/462, 457/461 and 461/466, all
  cross-chapter and all six words or more. **Drop those four and F21's eleven texts at twenty-two instances are what is
  left, exact. Ch 451's heading is three words long, so it sat under the floor either way.** The measurement was sound and
  simply had a floor below the thing it needed to see; F4 shows the phase read every `##` line in the volume, for
  vocabulary. Measured as headings, which is the measurement that was missing: **22 section-heading texts appear more
  than once across ch 421–478, at 45 instances, and exactly one collides inside a single chapter — this one.** The other
  21 are cross-chapter echoes and most are bookends rather than faults (`## The Shed` at ch 441 and ch 478, `## The
  Reading` at ch 445 and ch 473, `## The Yard At The Turn` at ch 421 and ch 428), and `## The Arithmetic` is the only
  text that appears three times, at ch 422, 429 and ch 460. **The repair is one heading and one line, and it is named here
  rather than made, because a close that improves a volume by writing prose is a close that has become a rewrite.**

### Where this leaves the run

**VOLUME 8 CLOSED AND AUDITED.** Twenty-two findings recorded, **none repaired, because a close that repairs is a
rewrite.** **The seventeen fixed items that carry the volume — the goal, the two days, the two brackets, the two costs,
the three shapes, the six mouths, Yuan's track, the four settlements and the pressure rotation — all PASS, and the close
review confirmed every one of them again.**
**THE REPAIRS A LATER PHASE SHOULD MAKE: SIXTY-SIX TO SIXTY-NINE line-level edits across TWENTY-SIX chapter files, plus twenty-nine trailing
newlines.** Twenty-six lines of calendar arithmetic across **ten** files; three card-vocabulary headings; thirty-three
straight apostrophes across fourteen files; a decision on `erased` at ch 424; three to five instances of `eighty-four` at
ch 464; **and one duplicated section heading at ch 451 (F22), whose file is already inside the apostrophe row.** *The
totals are corrected by the close review: `book-metadata.md` had published thirty-eight edits across thirty-one files
and this file sixty-six to sixty-eight across thirty-three, and neither survives.*

**THE VOLUME'S OWN ANSWER IS ON THE PAGE AND IT IS HERS, AND NO FINDING IN THIS AUDIT WEAKENS IT.** At ch 474 she reads
the document to the room before she refuses it — because nobody in that room can read it and she is the only person who
can — and then she says no, and a man of sixty-one writes one line and the date in the ordinary hand. **It went in.
There is no hand in that building or on that ground that takes it out. It is the fourth entry of her life and the first
one she chose.** And the price of it is not punishment: at ch 478 there is **nothing on nine days of road that wants
anything from her at all**, and it is the best thing that has happened to her in two years, and she chose it, and she
cannot say so out loud to anybody including him. **The month figures in F1, F2 and F3 are connective tissue and touch no
beat, no figure of hers, no refusal and no silence. Where the page is right, the page is right.**

**Volume 9 hands Volume 9 nothing of the Warden's office, the list, or the Anchor, because none of them is on Volume 8's
page.** What is on the page is: a shed with boards on the floor, three coppers, a refusal in the best book on nine days
of road that cannot be taken out, a piece of paper against her ribs she has not opened in four years, a voice that has
had nothing to spend for eighty-four days, and a woman who waited anyway. **`Shuyin` returns 0 across ch 421–478 and
nothing written here spends anything `outline/ending.md` §1 owns.**

### What Volume 9 must not lose — the hand-off, item by item, checked against ch 465–478

1. **She is NASCENT SOUL 9, the ceiling of a realm, at eighteen years old, and there is nothing above her.** Every technique she owns runs out at nine. Every resource she is able to hold runs out at nine. **There is no tenth and no hand anywhere on nine days of road or past the turn or on any road at all that can take her one step further.**
2. **The way out of a realm is a document, and the document is a piece of paper against her ribs.** It has been there four years, she put it there herself with her own hands, it says **Foundation the first stage** on the front of it, it has seven conditions referenced on the inside and a claim at the foot, and **she has still never opened it.** Volume 8 raised what it is worth three times (ch 470, ch 476, ch 478) **without ever reading it. The word `licence` appears three times across Volume 8 — at ch 425 and ch 449, both in Xie Changyin's mouth, both describing the Order's own paper — and zero times in Batch 0004. The relicensed licence against her ribs is never called anything and never opened.** She wants to open it. **She has not opened it, and the reason she has not is on the page and it is not fear: it is that at ch 470, in a room with a lamp in it, she worked out exactly what it is to have a document read aloud to you by somebody qualified to read it, and she is not going to be read to again.**
3. **Eight costs stand and none was re-derived or broken:** the furnace will not light itself · the hand cannot write a false line · a thing she reaches cannot be put back · she cannot be given anything · she cannot be paid · the reach answers a question and only a question · **she cannot call a halt — herself, once a thing she is in has started; she can still say no and cannot make the no take** · **there is nothing above her.**
4. **Body.** Four fingers and a thumb on the right hand. Right arm open half the time. Eight of eleven holds. Short breath four counts everywhere including inside a loaded reach. Aperture cracked through and permanent. **The small open place low behind her fourth rib, quiet since the twenty-first of a month four months back, came open on the eighteenth of the month beginning 1,274 and is not going to close.** Not hungry, and she has worked out on the page that this is not a gift. Eighteen.
5. **She refused the Order, on the record, at the fourth hour of the third of the month beginning 1,304, in a room with the shutters up, and a man of sixty-one wrote one line and the date in the ordinary hand.** **There is no hand in that building or on that ground that takes it out.** It was read back to her first — by Xie Changyin, and then, when she made her take back the one line that was hers, **by Ning Yan herself.** It is the fourth entry of her life and the first one she asked for.
6. **The cost of the refusal was not punishment. The Order stopped reading her.** The bench at the end of the lane went empty on the seventh, the floor stopped asking her about carts, the man behind the counter went back to asking her about nothing, the folder will go east on the first with nothing in it, and **at the last page nothing on nine days of road wants anything from her at all.** She said the mechanism out loud twice, on a bank and at a rail: **she bought her own anonymity with her own usefulness.**
7. **Yuan.** Hand. Readable without her, findable, held, sendable, **answerable and asked**. Three spends in Batch 0003 and **none since**. The fourth was put down at ch 462 and never spent. **The Hand was refused once out loud, in a room, at ch 471, with the reason given for the first time: *a hand only works where somebody's hand already is, and I cannot do the version where I am there to see what it does.*** **That refusal cost her nothing, and she found that worse than being taken from.** **He has had nothing to spend for eighty-four days and has said nothing since ch 471.** No figure of what is left of him is stated on any page in ch 465–478. The Ledger returns 0 and the word does not appear.
8. **The bill is on the page: one thousand one hundred and nine days and six hours**, the whole of it from the eighth of a month seven years back to the twenty-sixth of a month, given at ch 466 in the ordinary voice, in the present tense, once, after she asked for it. **Twenty-one days and four hours of it is what he put where a hand would find it — three times on this ground, and he told her about one of the three.** Twenty-six thousand six hundred and twenty-two hours; seventeen out of every hundred of an eighteen-year life; **one in fifty-three is everything she caught in seven weeks of counting.**
9. **And the finding she made out of it, which is the volume's own answer and is hers, not his:** **the standing draw is not for the work. The twenty-one days are the times he did a thing. The rest — one thousand and eighty-eight days and two hours — is the price of a person being the sort of person a place can put its hand on: the price of a voice in a furnace that somebody, once, from outside, could find.** **She did not find out who set the price, would not go through the door in it, and put it nowhere, because a column is a request.**
10. **The Year is still running and was mentioned zero times in fifty-eight chapters.** She does not forgive him and she does not tell him to stop. **The bill at ch 466 is a bill and not a forgiveness**: she did not thank him, did not argue it, and scraped it off a board with the point of a bar.
11. **What the Order is:** one building, one counter, one book with four hundred and seven names on it, one rate, one lamp on every table, one rule, and **no budget, which is Volume 12's.** **Its name is spoken once in the volume, at ch 425, in Xie Changyin's mouth in a shed at a mill.** No department, no floor, no rank list, no committee, no house, no title beyond *recruited*. **Its answer to why a bounded world needs readers is never given and the ch 267 surface answer is never re-voiced.**
12. **Xie Changyin, sixty-one, recruited at nineteen, forty-two years in, right about nine things in ten.** **She is on the page three times in this batch — the last day of the maximum at ch 470, the walk at ch 473 and the reading at ch 474 — and she has her own life now: the offer is final after the twelfth, she goes up the road for the season, and she has stopped arranging her season around Ning Yan.** She is **wrong about exactly one thing and does not know it: that a person on a roll is safer than a person off it.** Ning Yan refused that sentence and nothing else. **She does not learn the stop, the death, the two figures, the bill, or what the fine hand says about Ning Yan, and she is not told any of them.**
13. **The Order refused to be the thing that explains anything, and the ground acted by doing nothing at all — the first time in eight volumes that it has done nothing.** **Xie Changyin said, and it is the volume's last institutional word: *you have been entered four times in eight years and this is the first one you chose, and I am not going to congratulate you and I am not going to tell you that you were right.***
14. **The fine hand.** She can read one. **She cannot account for it** — she was not taught it and did not learn it anywhere, and at ch 474 she told a room full of strangers that and nothing else. **She has never been told what one says about her and never will be; that was given up on purpose at ch 417 and is not reopened, softened, queried or named in Volume 8.** A woman of twenty-two still does not know what a hand said about her, and a tied sheet with her on it went east on the twenty-second with nothing done to it.
15. **Live, carried, and not resolved:** the Hunter's request pending, both of his Volume 8 pages spent at 428 and 440, and he is **not** on the page in this batch · everything above the offices that vouched unread and unnamed · *kiln-born* in two heads and on no paper · what became of the eleven · the register still *reconciled* and unread · the plate still worn and undescribed · MEMBER ABSENT carried and never raised · the relicensing legs in order, none invented · the claim answered and not reopened · Hou Sanliang paid and never met · Ning Wu's decade a line with no mark · two yeses without meaning · **a chair at a crossing under a cloth with a light in the hut behind it** · **a man of thirty-four on a frame with two figures against one line of his own work that he has never once looked at** · **thirteen lines in a left column, two of them the layer's, that no one on that ground can explain** · **the address of a shelf, a room and a date on a string, given and not asked for, written on a board and scraped off and written again anyway** · **and the two sheets a woman wrote nine years ago and did not sign.**

### What Volume 9 opens onto, fixed by `outline/volume-08.md` and not re-derived here

**She has spent a volume being the strongest person in every room and it turned out to be a qualification; she has refused the one place on nine days of road that would have paid her for the only thing she is good at; the price of the refusal is that there is now nothing on nine days of road that wants anything from her; and at the top of a realm, with the only door in the world being a piece of paper she has been carrying since a furnace took her father, she is at last free enough to go and open it.** **The question is not whether she will be recruited again. It is what a person does with a silence that she bought, when the one voice in her head has nothing left to spend and has stopped answering, and there is a door eleven hundred years of practice has never been opened from the inside.**

**And the last thing that happened on the last page of Volume 8, which is the first thing Volume 9 opens on:** she said his name once into a shed with boards on the floor, and nothing came back, because he has had nothing to spend for eighty-four days and there is nothing left to answer with — **and she waited anyway.**

- **Ning Yan — NASCENT SOUL 9**, taken at ch 470 on the pinned day 1,291, the eighteenth of the month beginning 1,274, at the fourth hour, in a long room with the shutters up, **without her asking and without her being able to stop it. Bracket 2 of 2 in Volume 8.**
- **Hands and means.** **Three coppers in the fold in the inside of a coat, stitched shut, and bread for a day in a piece of paper.** She spent two coppers on one loaf at the store five miles west of the mill on the second day of her walk west, **because she wanted it**, and it was five down to three. Water out of her own skin; the trough is eleven miles from where she sleeps. Bar strap rebound with four inches off her own coat lining. A roof cord she tied herself. A door that does not shut properly that she did not fix. **Eight coppers remain a credit on Wei Ran's book and her hand will not take them and Wei Ran has said she will never chase them. Four coppers for the fine hand were swept back untaken at ch 447. The rate behind the counter is two coppers a page and the maximum offered was four coppers for every page, and her hand has never closed on either.**
- **Places she now is on nine days of road.** **The shed at the mill, four miles east of a mill whose wheel has not turned since before she came, and outside it a lifting floor with a rail and a board on it and forty-one lines.** Thirteen miles east: the lane, the cut, the frame, the room with six day books, the shed at the end of the lane, and the crossing a mile further with its painted board and its chain. Eleven miles further: the gate with the chain and the cup, and the long low lime-washed building with a lamp in every window and a counter at the near end. Nine days of road west: the mill, the counting house, the copying floor, the third floor, the room with the lamp, and Geng's lifting floor.
- **Untouched at the close and still true:** Feng 0 · Ning Wu 0 · Wen 0 · Shen Xiu 0 · Qiu, Mei Jiu, Du Hong, the sponsors 0 · **the year 0** · the register never read · **the plate named once, at ch 441, in Batch 0002, and never described** · **the relicensed licence never opened; the word `licence` stands three times in Volume 8 and both are the Order's own paper in Xie Changyin's mouth** · MEMBER ABSENT carried · *kiln-born* 0 · what became of the eleven not raised · the four months erased never named · the ch 128 silence unbroken · the ch 150 refusal not softened · no second Lung and no second appointment · the claim not reopened · Hou paid and never met · **her mother's name never said** · `Ledger` 0 · **no harem and nothing a romance** · nothing of the ending previewed · no budget, no hall, no department, no floor, no rank list, no committee, no house, no title beyond *recruited* · **the surface answer not re-voiced and the tenth part not given** · no death in Volume 8 Batch 0004 and no office above the copying floor named anywhere in the volume.

- **Seven costs stand, none re-derived:** the furnace will not light itself · the hand cannot write a false line · a thing reached cannot be put back · she cannot be given anything · she cannot be paid · the reach answers a question and only a question · she cannot call a halt.

- **Money: nine → five coppers on day 1,209, two loaves at two coppers a loaf, and five unchanged to the close.** No bread since the twenty-eighth of the month before last, which is day 1,211. *Both figures corrected at the Volume 8 Batch 0003 review; the drafting phase had day 1,208 and the eleventh, and neither re-derives from the chapters.* Water in her skin and the trough at seven miles.

## Who is where at end of chapter 449 — VOLUME 8 BATCH 0002 WRITTEN
