## Who is where at end of chapter 586 — VOLUME 10 BATCH 0003 WRITTEN (cold reader starts here)

Full record: `state/continuity.md` under ***Volume 10 Batch 0003 (ch 571–586) — established facts, carried to Batch
0004***, ***Construction budget — Volume 10 Batch 0003*** and ***Volume 10 Batch 0003 — REVIEW REPAIR***. **Day
  1,771, the eighteenth of the month beginning 1,754, twenty-three days after she said the name and thirty-one days from
  the closing.** **One convention warning for whoever reads the figures below: Batch 0003's totals were re-measured at
  this repair and reproduce exactly under `wc -w` over non-`#` lines. Batches 0001 and 0002 were not re-measured, and
  their published totals (29,678 and 27,575) sit 157 and 176 words under what that same command returns now (29,835 and
  27,751). Those two figures are carried forward untouched rather than quietly restated, and the Volume 10 close should
  re-measure all forty-eight chapters under one method.**

- **Ning Yan — SEVERANCE 5, taken at ch 572 on day 1,697, at the pinned 68-day interval.** Cost, named once, **and it is
  on the page in her own mouth at ch 572 in a new section headed *What The Fifth Stage Took*, with its working shown
  again at ch 573**: **a person who tells her where somebody is hands her the place they were, and she cannot walk toward
  one.** A name used to be a road and it stopped being a road in nine seconds. She is nineteen. Short breath four counts
  everywhere including a loaded reach, and seven once on made road at the third hour in the last nine days. Four fingers
  and a thumb on the right hand; the right index gone. Right arm open four days in five, and six days in seventeen after
  she came back. Eight of eleven holds. A small open place low behind her fourth rib, the size of a coin, open since the
  eighteenth of the month beginning 1,544, and nothing has come out of it since the twenty-sixth of the month beginning
  1,604. **She is lighter than she has ever been and has been lighter every single day since that eighteenth without one
  day of it going back. She is not hungry and has not been since that eighteenth.**
- **What she is at the last page.** Standing at the head of a lane at the seventh hour of the eighteenth of the month
  beginning 1,754 with a bar on her shoulder, having just said her brother's name out loud into an empty yard where two
  people could hear it if either of them turned round. **She had no bread at all for the whole of the twenty-three days
  after the naming and did not eat, and she has not eaten since she came back from the wall.**
- **What happened in the twenty-three days, because the batch now says so.** Seventeen of them are on the page in one
  place, at ch 586. **She stayed at the post. She asked one question in thirty-three days — *when does the next board go
  down* — on the second of the month beginning 1,754, and the woman said the second, and that was the last day on which
  stone of hers is ever paid for.** She worked the face on the fourth and the fifth anyway and got nothing. On the
  eleventh a board came down the strip road and the boy of eleven carried the near end and neither of them said one word,
  **and she decided standing on the flat stone that she is going to spend one mouth carefully.** On the fourteenth she
  walked east and stood two hours and twenty minutes in the yard of the shut door and did not open it, **and there was
  fresh lime under the bar that had not been there on the fourth of the month beginning 1,694, pale and loose and
  unswept-to-set, ground into the barrow-way where somebody had swept it out and stepped back in. Somebody has been out
  of that house inside four days. Nobody on nine days of road knows it but her and she does not know what the thing was.**
- **The bill, and it is the only one.** **She said Ning Wu at the seventh hour of the twenty-fifth of the month
  beginning 1,724, in daylight, four feet into an opening in a wall, on her own side, with a boy of fifteen four feet
  off behind her and a carter four hundred yards east.** A man of nine hundred years had a brush in a bag behind him
  and **did not turn round and get it.** It cannot be un-said. **Nothing was bought. `Ning Wu` is on the page twice in
  sixteen chapters and is not in nine hundred years and will not be true on the closing day.**
- **What it bought, exactly one thing.** She can say it again. Any day. It costs nothing. **It is the only thing she
  has said in her life that she can put down**, and she is not going to be able to put this one down either, and it
  does not matter, because it was never going to be in a book anyway.
- **Four questions standing in a book nine hundred years deep and none answered.** What else is on it · what it is for ·
  what does the word do · will you tell me when, answered *there is a day*. **The first was asked on the twenty-fourth of
  the month beginning 1,604, day 1,627, and has been standing a hundred and twenty-one days** and she is not allowed a
  fifth. **She found the lever — asking twice — on the nineteenth of a month beginning 1,634, day 1,652, and has carried
  it on purpose for a hundred and nineteen days and has not pulled it, including on the twenty-fifth in the best hour of
  her life.** One anchor, one number: the lever's discovery is dated the nineteenth of a month beginning 1,634
  everywhere on the page and nowhere else.
- **What she is carrying.** A bar nine feet nine · a skin · a bag with a dish, a lump of clay, a ring a finger's
  width, a stub of a nib and a cake of ink she has not used in two months · **a book of eight leaves made out of a
  blanket inside her coat against the side where the paper is** — a date and a place on the first leaf, four sentences
  on the second she will not read again, **six empty ones that are hers and that she has decided not to write in, and
  she put a nib to the first of the six at the fourth hour of the twenty-eighth of the month beginning 1,724 and put
  it away** — and the paper itself, opened with her own two hands four years ago and never once called anything.
  **There are two things against her ribs and she has not called either of them anything.**
- **The flat stone under the bottom of the ladder is bare** and has been bare since she took her own book off it with
  her own two hands. **Nothing in this batch puts anything on it.**
- **What she has for it.** A stone face at the head of a lane four miles west of the turn, thirteen miles from the
  post, measured by the weight in a barrow and paid at the store woman's rate against a board that goes down to the
  store **on the second day of a month**. **To be paid she has to be at the face on the first and at the store on the
  second, and she cannot be at the face on the first and at a wall two miles past a gate on the first.** She has no
  wages, no place, no shed and no road.
- **The geography, fixed and not to be re-derived.** The post is nine miles west of the turn · **a lane leaves the
  store road at the turn and runs four miles west to the stone face, and at forty feet into that lane there is a low
  house with a bar across the door, a standing stone in the yard, and a woman of fifty** · **the store road runs four
  miles west from the turn to a cottage with a shut door at the mouth of a stopped lime bed; the mill is a mile beyond
  it at five with a wheel that has not turned for nine years; the store is behind the mill at fourteen miles from the
  post** · the bar on two posts is four hundred yards east of the turn · the wall is twenty-two miles east of the post,
  nine of strip, eleven of made, two past a gate, **and there is nothing between the gate and the wall to lie down on**.
- **The terms.** One at a time · at the wall · in daylight. They do not move. Twenty-two miles each way, forty-four a
  question, two days.
- **The one road there is.** A carter came down that road with a board nine years back, put four names on it and took
  them east, and the four went east with it, and the carter came back without them and was not the next one back for a
  long time. **The miller says the boy went with them and walked behind the last barrow because he would not get up on
  one.** That is nine years old and it is the only road on this ground that is in the past tense about him. **Nothing
  on the page places him and he has never been on this volume's page in person.**
- **Him.** `Yuan` **0** by name across sixteen chapters and on the page **once**, at ch 584, alone, in a reading-space,
  in the present tense, with no other character in it and **no figure of what is left of him stated**. He opens no wall,
  is wrong-sided from day 1,521, is findable and sendable and answerable, and is not thanked anywhere. **His frame
  answer is on the page: a name cannot be held open past the closing except by somebody left permanently on the wrong
  side of it, and the only such person on nine days of road is her.** He put that in front of her in daylight, before
  she said the name.
- **Shuyin** — receives at the wall twice, at ch 580 and ch 585, and never walks west. `Shuyin` **0** by name in these
  sixteen chapters. `Warden` **0**. **He did not write the name and did not say whether it was true. He is alive, old,
  correct, not thanked, and he shuts the book at ch 596–597.**
- **The Hunter, Wei Ran, the Order, the Sisterhood, the bargain, Feng, Xie Changyin, Shen Xiu** — none of them is on
  the page in these sixteen chapters. **The Hunter's request stays pending.**
- **Untouched and unraised:** the window a hundred yards off the wall, **open and unlearned, its four questions
  unspent and now known to cost a trip** · ***twenty-two days*, still OPEN and unplaced** · what *lost* does ·
  the paper against her ribs, never called anything · the register still *reconciled* · the plate still worn and
  undescribed · *kiln-born* in two heads on no paper · what became of the eleven not raised · the four months never
  named · the ch 150 refusal never softened · the surface answer never re-voiced · `Ledger` **0** · **the year of
  non-forgiveness not mentioned once in sixteen chapters** · no harem and nothing a romance · **she never says her
  mother's name** · no page of his book read out loud and no name ever read · **no bracket anywhere but the one at
  ch 572.**
- **The one thank you she said in this batch was to him**, at ch 580, for four sentences about a house, **and it
  bought nothing and she did not expect it to buy anything.** She has not thanked the woman of fifty, the woman of
  sixty, the woman behind the counter or the boy of fifteen.

- **Ning Yan — SEVERANCE 6, taken at ch 588 on day 1,772, the nineteenth of the month beginning 1,754, at the pinned
  75-day interval from Severance 5 at day 1,697.** Cost, named once and ON THE PAGE in her own mouth at ch 588 in the
  section *What The Sixth Stage Took*: **she cannot keep what she finds by holding it.** Shown working three times — her
  own heelprint ninety seconds old · the room behind a window · **nine coppers counted four times on a store counter
  and four answers.** A finding holds about two hours in her and the only way to keep one is to walk it while she has
  it. **She is nineteen.** Four fingers and a thumb on the right hand, the right index gone. Right arm open four days in
  five. Eight of eleven holds. Four counts of breath, and seven on a rise and inside a loaded reach. A small open place
  low behind her fourth rib, the size of a coin, open since the eighteenth of the month beginning 1,544, and the merit
  came out of it at ch 588 and has not come out since. **She is lighter than she has ever been and has been lighter every
  single day since that eighteenth without one day of it going back. She is not hungry and has not been since that
  eighteenth.**
- **What she is at the last page.** Standing at the turn on the store road at the dusk of the seventeenth of the month
  beginning 1,814, **day 1,830**, with a bar on her shoulder and a bag and nothing else, having watched a brother of
  nineteen walk west past her at four hundred yards, stop at six feet, say he has not been in a mill either, and go on.
- **What the closing did, and what it cost, in the order the volume took it.** The last asking at ch 595 on **day 1,802,
  the nineteenth of the month beginning 1,784**, at the seventh hour, in daylight, at the wall, with the boy of fifteen
  four feet off behind her and a carter two miles west of the gate. **She asked the question she asked first — what else
  is on it, standing since day 1,627 — and he answered the smallest thing on the page, and the answer was not a name.
  It was a number. Six.** She had counted **four** go over a chain at the fourth hour of the twelfth and had thought the
  number was four.
- **IRREVERSIBLE, ch 596.** He shut the book on a plank table in daylight at the ninth hour of the closing day, with
  nothing on it, in about four seconds, and his hands stayed on the cover for a count of thirty. **Neither of them
  asked the other for anything.** No page read, no name read, nothing shown of what shutting does, and nothing on that
  ground changed while it happened. **It cannot be un-shut.**
- **IRREVERSIBLE, ch 598.** **She found Ning Wu alive, in person, in her hearing, once**, at the first hour of the first
  of the month beginning 1,814, day 1,814, working barrows in a ground beyond the opening. **Nineteen years old, nine
  years in that ground, fed, with hands like her father's, not surprised and not glad.** His first words were *you are
  shorter*. **He does not forgive her and has not: *you can come, it is not the same as being let.*** He does not know
  what a list is and does not know what *lost* means. **She had four days and a night and got nothing out of it that she
  could put anywhere. It settles nothing and it cannot be un-found.**
- **Three questions standing in a shut book** and no fourth and no fifth: *what is it for* (thirtieth of the month
  beginning 1,604) · *what does the word do* (fifteenth of the month beginning 1,634) · *will you tell me when*,
  answered *there is a day*. **There is no book in the world she is permitted to put a question into.** Her own entry
  is in the shut book as *lost* and no shut book can take it out, and when she said *I am going to take it out* a
  nineteen-year-old asked *with what* and she did not have an answer.
- **The lever — asking twice.** Written in the dust at the fourth foot of an opening on the eighteenth of the month
  beginning 1,784, **rubbed out and not written again**, after a hundred and forty-eight days of carrying it on purpose.
  **Not pulled anywhere in this batch.** He said *I would not have stopped you* and she said *I know that*.
- **What she is carrying at the last page.** A bar nine feet nine · a skin · a bag with a dish, a lump of clay, a ring a
  finger’s width, a stub of a nib and a cake of ink she has not used in two months · **nine coppers she cannot count**,
  paid for nine barrows on the second of the month beginning 1,784 · a sheet of paper against her ribs opened with her
  own two hands four years ago and never once called anything · **a book of eight leaves made out of a blanket with a
  date and a place on the first leaf, four sentences on the second she will not read again, and six leaves that are
  hers and that STAY BLANK.** **There are two things against her ribs and she has called neither of them anything.**
- **The flat stone under the bottom of the ladder is bare** and nothing in Volume 10 puts anything on it.
- **Geography, fixed and not to be re-derived.** The post is nine miles west of the turn · the turn is thirteen miles
  west of the wall · **the chain is where the strip road ends and the made road begins, at a burn in a cut nine feet
  deep with three planks, kept by an unnamed man of fifty-five who took it over because the man who had it before is
  alive and does not come back; it is opened in daylight for anybody who asks and is not opened at night** · the wall is
  twenty-two miles east of the post and **nothing between the gate and the wall to lie down on** · **and beyond the
  opening, a made road running east, rows of barrows with a stone at the head of each, about two hundred people, no man
  over any of them, nobody paid, and not one person in nine years has come back out of it.**
- **THE VOICE, gone over on the page once at ch 600 and never explained, and not named anywhere in Volume 10.** Something in
  her head has counted with her for eight years, has
  read out every figure anybody hands her before she has finished taking it in, and got a number wrong once and
  corrected itself in her head before she had finished being wrong. **It has never once said what it was before it
  started counting.** She has had the question since the month beginning 1,574 and has never put it anywhere.
  **She does not ask him yet. He is still talking.** That is the last line of the volume.
- **Shuyin.** Shut the book, walked thirteen miles west with her over five days, said *you do not have a road, go east*,

  the finding and is applied rather than re-derived).
- **What the closing did.** She asked the last question at the seventh hour of **day 1,802, the nineteenth of the month
  beginning 1,784**, four feet into an opening, in daylight, with a boy of fifteen four feet off behind her and a carter
  two miles west of the gate. **He answered the smallest thing on the page and the answer was not a name. It was a
  number. Six.** She had counted **four** go over a chain at the fourth hour of the twelfth and had thought the number
  was four.
- **IRREVERSIBLE, ch 596.** He shut the book on a plank table in daylight at the ninth hour of the closing day, with
  nothing on it, in about four seconds, his hands staying on the cover for a count of thirty. **Neither of them asked the
  other for anything.** No page read, no name read, nothing shown of what shutting does, and nothing on that ground
  changed while it happened. **It cannot be un-shut.**
- **IRREVERSIBLE, ch 598.** **She found Ning Wu alive, in person, in her hearing, once**, at the first hour of the first
  of the month beginning 1,814, working barrows in a ground beyond the opening. Nineteen years old, nine years there,
  fed, with hands like her father's, not surprised and not glad. **His first words were *you are shorter*. He does not
  forgive her and has not: *you can come, it is not the same as being let.*** He does not know what a list is and does
  not know what *lost* means. **She had four days and a night and got nothing out of it that she could put anywhere. It
  settles nothing and it cannot be un-found.**
- **`Ning Wu` is 6 across the volume, in six chapters.** Named in her own want at ch 550 and ch 566 · said out loud at a
  wall at ch 568, ch 572 and ch 585, where **he does not write and says nothing for four minutes** · and in person at
  ch 598. **No page of the list ever has his name in it.**
- **Three questions standing in a shut book** and no fourth and no fifth: *what is it for* (thirtieth of the month
  beginning 1,604) · *what does the word do* (fifteenth of the month beginning 1,634) · *will you tell me when*, answered
  *there is a day*. **There is no book in the world she is permitted to put a question into.** Her own entry is in the
  shut book as *lost* and no shut book can take it out, and when she said *I am going to take it out* a nineteen-year-old
  asked *with what* and she did not have an answer.
- **The lever — asking twice.** Written in the dust at the fourth foot of an opening on the eighteenth of the month
  beginning 1,784, **rubbed out and not written again**, after a hundred and forty-eight days of carrying it on purpose.
  **Not pulled anywhere in the volume.** He said *I would not have stopped you* and she said *I know that*.
- **Geography, fixed and not to be re-derived. post → 9 miles of strip road → turn → 11 miles of made road → gate → 2
  miles → wall → the opening. Twenty-two miles end to end, and twenty-two miles back.** The turn is thirteen miles west
  of the wall. The bar on two posts is four hundred yards east of the turn, and the wheel-marks run from it to a quarter of
  a mile beyond the opening, **thirteen miles**. **The chain is where the strip road ends and the made road begins, a burn
  in a cut nine feet deep with three planks, kept by an unnamed man of fifty-five who took it over because the man who had
  it before is alive and does not come back; it is opened in daylight for anybody who asks and is not opened at night.**
  **Beyond the opening, a made road running east, rows of barrows with a stone at the head of each, about two hundred
  people, no man over any of them, nobody paid, no board and no chalk, and not one person in nine years has come back out
  of it. There is nothing between the gate and the wall to lie down on.**
  **ONE THING VOLUME 11 MUST NOT TAKE AS SETTLED:** ch 560 places the lane four miles west of the turn and thirteen miles
  from the post in three consecutive sentences that cannot all be true, and ch 585 and ch 600 both list the lane as four
  miles west. **The close recorded the conflict and did not choose a map.**
- **THE VOICE, gone over on the page once at ch 600 and never explained, and not named anywhere in Volume 10.** Something in
  her head has counted with her for eight years, has
  read out every figure anybody hands her before she has finished taking it in, and got a number wrong once and
  corrected itself in her head before she had finished being wrong. **It has never once said what it was before it started
  counting.** She has had the question since the month beginning 1,574 and has never put it anywhere. **She does not ask
  him yet. He is still talking. That is the last line of Volume 10.**
- **Him.** `Yuan` is **not named anywhere in the volume**. He is on the page **once, at ch 596**, in one sentence:
  *He is not going to stop it and I did not ask him to.* **No figure of what is left of him is stated before or after
  either irreversible step.** He opens no wall and is wrong-sided from day 1,521. **He is not thanked.**
- **Shuyin.** `Shuyin` **3 by name in the whole volume** — ch 541, ch 563, ch 565, all of them her narration and never his
  own mouth. `Warden` **0**. He shuts the book, walks thirteen miles west to the turn with her in five days, says *you do
  not have a road, go east*, and goes east. **Alive, old, correct, and he returns.** The wall stayed open behind him and
  nobody shut it. **RECORDED FINDING, NOT REPAIRED: he is thanked once, at ch 580, in daylight at the west jamb at the
  eighth hour of the twenty-seventh of the month beginning 1,694, for four sentences about a house — and ch 596 and
  ch 600 both carry that forward. `outline/volume-10.md` says *alive, old, and not thanked.* The page and the outline
  disagree and the close did not reconcile them.**
- **Wei Ran** is on the page in **two chapters, ch 543 and ch 589**, `Wei Ran` **15 times, on word boundaries**, against an
  allowance of two cards and Volume 9's real three. Carried twice at a rate of six coppers the four miles, **the number
  said out loud both times**, one opinion, no gift, no romance.
- **The Hunter** is **never named** — `Hunter` is **0** across the volume — and is on the page **once**, at ch 567,
  described rather than named, nine feet off, with a notebook shut under his arm, and she asks him and he does not know.
  **His request stays pending.** His Batch 0004 card in the outline was not spent.
- **Untouched and unraised, each a fact and not a topic.** The window a hundred yards off the east side — **OPEN, four
  questions unspent, approached at ch 591 and nothing asked** · **the lime under the bar of the shut door — LEFT,
  unspent, turned into nothing** · ***twenty-two days* — STILL OPEN, put in front of her again at ch 590 and equated to
  nothing** · a hut light left standing, still unlearned · the paper against her ribs, never named · the register still
  *reconciled* · the plate still worn and undescribed · *kiln-born* in two heads on no paper · what became of the eleven
  not raised · the four months never named · the ch 150 refusal never softened · the surface answer never re-voiced ·
  `Ledger` **0** · **the year of non-forgiveness not mentioned once** · no harem and nothing a romance · **she never says
  her mother's name** · no page of his book read out loud and no name ever read · **no bracket anywhere but the one at
  ch 588.**
- **What the close repaired, in one line:** eighteen sentences and two lines across eleven files — a drafter's scratch note
  printed as body prose at ch 592 and removed, five distances put back onto the volume's own nine/eleven/two spine, and
  eleven narrator's counts of a structural mouth corrected to the truth without a word of any mouth being touched.

**This is the live position of the manuscript. `chapters/volume-11/` holds ch 601–615, fifteen chapters, 29,284 words of

ch 616–630. `outline/volume-11.md` is the authority and none of it is re-planned here.

- **DAY 1,841, the twenty-eighth of the month beginning 1,814, at the turn on the store road, at the ninth hour.**
  `((1,841 − 134) mod 30) + 1 = 28`. **Severance 7 falls at ch 616 on day 1,842 and this batch takes no bracket: zero
  bracketed layer lines in fifteen chapters.**
- **A bar nine feet nine** on her left shoulder · a skin · a bag with a dish, a lump of clay and a ring a finger's width,
  a stub of a nib and a cake of ink she has not used in two months · **one copper she cannot count** and a quarter of a
  loaf, three-quarters of it gone · a sheet of paper against her ribs opened with her own two hands four years back and
  never once called anything · **a book of eight leaves made out of a blanket, six of them hers and STILL BLANK.**
  **There are two things against her ribs and she has called neither of them anything.**
- **SEVERANCE 6**, taken at ch 588 on day 1,772. Six stages past the ceiling. **Nineteen years old.** Four fingers and a
  thumb, the index gone · right arm open four days in five and open again at the ninth hour on the twenty-sixth ·
  eight of eleven holds · four counts of breath, seven on a rise and inside a loaded reach · **a small open place low
  behind her fourth rib, the size of a coin, open since the eighteenth of the month beginning 1,544, which is day 1,561;
  the figure at any day D is D − 1,560, which is 281 on the day of ch 615, and not one of them going back. Not hungry.**
- **THE THREE VOLUME 10 COSTS, carried and applied, never re-derived:** she cannot be counted where she stands ·
  **she cannot follow a name she is given** (applied at ch 612 to the carter’s board, which gave four names east nine
  years back) · **she cannot keep what she finds by holding it** (applied at ch 611 to her own work: eleven barrows,
  eleven, eleven, twelve, eleven).
- **WHAT THIS BATCH COST HER, in order.** Nine coppers that would not count → two coppers a loaf → six coppers the four
  miles → **one copper.** Four miles west to a shut door she did not open, one mile short of her brother, and four miles
  back on an arm that went in the first mile. A brother going west at the eighth hour of the twenty-second, five
  sentences in nine years, three of them about bread. **A count that missed on money for the first time in ten years.**
  Eleven barrows on the best day she has had in two years and no way to keep the figure.
- **WHAT THIS BATCH CHANGED.** **She asked him, out loud, alone, at the ninth hour of the twenty-fourth of the month
  beginning 1,814, at day 1,837: *What were you before you started counting.*** **IRREVERSIBLE AND IT CANNOT BE
  UN-ASKED.** He did not answer. He went careful. **He named the terms: one at a time · in daylight.** They do not move
  for the rest of the volume. **The wall is gone, so a question is done standing: eleven days each way to nowhere.**
- **THE LEVER.** Asking twice. Carried whole, taken out in the open at the sixth hour of the twenty-eighth, **and
  declined — at ch 609 and again at ch 614.** It is not pulled anywhere in this batch. The reason is given on the page:
  **a thing you can ask twice is a thing you can be refused twice, and she wants a thing she can ask once.**
- **THE ANSWER SO FAR — ch 615, the twenty-eighth of the month beginning 1,814, in daylight, at the seventh hour, out
  loud, alone, on open ground, with the turn for a witness.** Her question was not the one from the twenty-fourth and not
  twice: **what did you do on an ordinary day.** He answered in four seconds in the present tense and gave her **three
  things where she asked for one: a furnace, a number, and nineteen.** *Bricks. Two hundred and forty courses in a day
  at six hundred and forty to a course. I was the one who counted them. I have been counting bricks since I was eleven
  years old and I have never once been paid for it.* A furnace east of here, **not this sect and not any sect she has
  stood in.** And nineteen — *those are two different sentences and you are going to have to work out which one of them
  you believed just now.* **She did not take a fourth thing. One at a time.**
- **FIRST ORIGIN MOUTH, ch 608, a furnace-man’s mate of forty-five with his hands burned into the creases, six
  sentences across the chapter, none of them a speech.** The boy weighed the lime barrows before they went in · nobody
  pays him, his father fires · **a boy who counts what nobody is paying him to count is a boy the furnace will use** ·
  it is not the same thing as keeping and not one man at a fire thinks it is, and the only people who found the
  difference out were the ones it was done to · it happened without anybody meaning it and not one man there was cruel ·
  **he had a name and nobody at that fire used it, and she did not ask again.** He told her he had told this to two
  people in twenty years and she was the third. **The tenth part is not given and nothing proves the crews wrong.**
  **`Yuan Sheng` is 0 across the batch and the mate does not know the name.**
- **Geography, unchanged and re-derived by use, not re-planned.** post → 9 of strip → turn → 11 of made → gate → 2 →
  wall, twenty-two miles end to end; the bar on two posts is four hundred yards east of the turn with nothing on its

  four miles west to a stone face with a woman of fifty at forty feet; **the store road runs four miles west to a
  cottage with a shut door at a stopped lime bed, the mill is a mile beyond it at five with a wheel that has not turned
  in nine years, the store is behind the mill and is fourteen miles from the post.** **The lane is four miles west of

- **`Ning Wu` is 0 by name across the fifteen chapters.** He is walked past once, at ch 605, on the store road at the

## VOLUME 11 BATCH 0001 — REVIEW REPAIR APPLIED (no prose restarted, no plot moved)

**Thirteen findings from `logs/volume-11-batch-0001.review.log` are closed in place. The fifteen chapters, the volume
arc, the ch 609 irreversible step, the three Volume 10 costs, the terms, the lever and the five origin mouths are all
where they were. `workspace/volume-11-batch-0002/PROMPT.md` remains the one next phase and was edited in place, not
re-dispatched. No new phase directory and no `.done` marker.**

### The five figures the next drafter inherits, all now derived rather than carried

1. **The turn to the wall is THIRTEEN MILES — eleven of made road to the gate and two past it.** The batch had
   thirteen in ch 603 and twenty-two in four other chapters. **Twenty-two is the POST-to-wall distance** (nine of
   strip road to the turn plus thirteen), which is how `state/current.md` has always recorded it, and the two were
   being used for the same walk. **Every twenty-two in `chapters/volume-11/` is now thirteen.** Nothing between the
   gate and the wall to lie down on, and a round trip no longer fits in a day.
2. **The keeper's post is NINE MILES WEST of the turn, past the mill.** Volume 10 canon, stated four times (ch 539,
   543, 555, 560, 561) and already in `state/current.md`. Ch 601 and ch 607 had it east and would have canonised the
   error into ch 616–630 through the next prompt. Both are west now, and the next prompt is corrected twice.
3. **HER STANDING CLOCK. The turn runs the EIGHTEENTH to the TWENTY-NINTH of the month beginning 1,814 — day 1,831
   to day 1,842 — and the twenty-ninth is the day of SEVERANCE 7.** *Days of standing left* means days remaining
   **after** today: eighteenth eleven · twenty-first eight · twenty-second seven · twenty-third six · twenty-fourth
   five · twenty-fifth four · twenty-sixth three · twenty-seventh two · twenty-eighth one. The draft had the end of
   standing landing on four different days. It is the twenty-ninth, once.
4. **THE BOARD CLOCK. A board goes down on the second of a month and she must be at a face on the first.** The next
   one is the second of the next month, **day 1,845**: **fourteen days off the eighteenth, eleven off the twenty-first,
   ten off the twenty-second, nine off the twenty-third, six off the twenty-sixth, three off the twenty-eighth.**
   Standing runs out three days before the work does, which is what ch 601 says and every other chapter now matches.
5. **THE GOING-LIGHTER, unchanged and unchallenged. The figure at any day D is D − 1,560**, and it is the
   **eighteenth of the month beginning 1,544** = day 1,561 that anchors it, on twenty-two Volume 10 pages. Every
   figure in the batch equals its own day and none was carried down.

### What else was corrected, and what was changed versus what was only dated

- **ch 612 — the date sum, load-bearing for four plot facts.** The chapter had *nine years back from the twenty-seventh
  of the month beginning 1,814 is the eighteenth of the month beginning 1,544*, which is false three ways. Canon
  fixes the place behind her fourth rib at the eighteenth of a month beginning 1,544, so the chapter's own date was
  wrong, not the aperture. **Nine years back from the twenty-seventh of the month beginning 1,814 is the twenty-seventh
  of the month beginning 1,805, and the lime, the wheel and the boy behind the last barrow are all on THAT date.**
  **Nine years is now two dates nine days apart and the chapter is called *The Two Dates*.** The finding got sharper,
  not thinner: the boy and the hole in her are not on one line, and nine days is the width of a barrow track.
- **ch 606 and ch 614 — inherited dating.** The question she has carried since *the month beginning 1,574* is now
  **the fifteenth of the month beginning 1,634**, the date ch 606's own list gives for the question that got no
  answer back, which is the moment the chapter describes. **"Eight questions in eight years" is now eight questions**
  — the span runs 1,604 to 1,784 and cannot also be eight years. The voice's eight years are untouched.
- **ch 615 — the rule's age.** *She had made the rule on the sixth stage and carried it for nine years* is now **it
  was sixty-nine days old.** Severance 6 is at day 1,772 and ch 615 is day 1,841.
- **ch 608 — the chapter ran backwards.** *What She Asked Last* sat at the eighth hour before *What He Asked Her* at
  the seventh. The two sections are now in order. The exchange that gave **he** two different antecedents across two
  lines — *He was ten.* / *That is the age.* — is rewritten as *The boy at your fire was ten.* / *Ten.*
- **ch 608 and the six sentences.** The man speaks a dozen times in the chapter and four later chapters quote *six
  sentences* as a carried artifact. **Ch 608 now counts them on the page** — the six that are not an answer to a
  figure and are not a trade — so the number is true where it is used. The six are listed and they are the six.
- **Titles.** Three were not names: *Eight Miles On A Bad Arm* → **The Mile She Did Not Walk** · *Six Coppers The Four
  Miles* → **A Rate With An End In It** · *The Eighteenth Of A Month* (a date, banned outright) → **The Two Dates**.
  All fifteen remain unique against 615 headings.
- **Three endings closed on a recap rather than a hook** and now close on movement: **ch 602** ends on the two
  figures neither of them can put back, **ch 610** ends on the three mornings going round in her head with no sleep,
  **ch 613** ends on going to the sixth hour with nothing on any leaf. The *lighter at the X hour than the Y, which is
  N days of it* construction now runs seven times in fifteen chapters and closes **no** chapter — it is strongest

- **SEVERANCE 7.** Six stages past the ceiling and one more. **Nineteen years old.** Four fingers and a thumb, the

- **WHAT THIS BATCH COST HER.** **A board on the second of the month beginning 1,844 that went down a day east of the
  gate.** A heel of loaf that went on the twenty-seventh of a month beginning 1,844. Nine days walking on a sentence
  a woman said to her in the road. **Two hundred yards of rows she stood outside and did not go into, a quarter of a
  mile off, at the ninth hour and forty minutes of the ninth of a month beginning 1,844, and sixty-one rows counted
  before the light went.** A bar of fire four feet over her head at ch 629 because she did not hear a man shouting.
  **The cruelest sentence she has ever said to a person, on a woman’s own step, where four men work, at ch 621.**
  **And the last of it: her own asking at ch 609 put the knowing into the world and the ground closed, and she

- **WHAT THIS BATCH CHANGED.** **SEVERANCE 7, at ch 616, and the cost: she cannot hear what she is told about him —

- **THE SECOND ORIGIN MOUTH, ch 622, six short sentences in five speeches, none of them a speech.** A harvest taken
  for a stalled ascension is written down truly by people who did not choose it, **and the writing saved nobody and
  nobody there has ever said it did.** **The tenth part is not given and nothing proves the crews wrong.**
- **THE VOLUME MIDPOINT IS SPENT AT ch 630.** The harvest named truly by the widow's page — a senior at the first

  that is not on the page, for the first time in his life: the fire did not take all of it, and the part that woke up
  is the part that survived.** **And it does not hold her.** Every figure in the entry comes back for the rest of her
  life and the morning does not, **because she was not in it.** **And the page has no place on it — a book like that
  assumes everybody who reads it is already standing in the room — and she has been on that ground fifty-nine days
  and has not found which part of it.**
- **THE TERMS, one at a time · in daylight · standing. They do not move.** The wall is gone; a question is done
  standing. **The lever — asking twice — is carried and declined and is not pulled anywhere in this batch.**
- **THE PEOPLE ON THAT GROUND, all correct and none thanked.** A widow of forty-five with a shut book and two
  sentences said out loud in nineteen years · **a boy of fifteen who calls every figure out loud, the way she does,
  and brought her the coarse charcoal without a word** · a man of fifty-one with a board and a good hand · a man of
  forty with a long bar · nine years of a row of barrows nose up against a knee-high wall · a furnace that has not
  been let out in nineteen years. **The ground did not want her and let her stay and has gone quiet on her, and she
  can count exactly when.**
- **`Ning Wu` is 0 by name across the batch.** She stood outside the rows on the ninth of the month beginning 1,844
  and did not go in and did not call out and did not say his name. **He does not know what a list is and does not
  forgive her.** **His one meeting in Batch 0001 is spent and this batch owes him nothing.**
- **The man with the shut notebook is on the page once, at ch 619, and she asked him.** He said he did not know, and
  then said that it was the fourth question anybody had ever asked him that he would rather not answer, and one
  thing on the way down a slope: **a true thing is not the same as a thing that is yours.** **She never put the name
  in his hearing.** `Hunter` 0 on word boundaries.
- **Wei Ran, Shuyin, Feng Lushi, `Feng`, `Scribe` and `Ledger` are all 0 across the batch.** `Scribe` remains at 0 of

  used to carry, *not mentioned once*, was wrong and is corrected here. Nobody is thanked.

  thirteen people she did not ask to be there, that the tax on that ground was designed to find people like her. He

  it, and she is not going to walk out of the fifth place in ten years.**

  her at a gate and refused a fourth, which is a name. **`Scribe` is spent: one naming in the whole volume.**

  what she finds by holding it · she cannot be given anything · she cannot be paid · the reach answers a question and

  years at a work on himself, **a man of seventy-one called Accountant who kept the harvest book and will not open it
  again, and a man of fifty with ash on both forearms who pushes the barrow out at the second hour.** The furnace has
  not been let out in nineteen years.

- **Ning Yan** — **SEVERANCE 9**, taken at the tenth hour of day 1,997, the fourth of the month beginning 1,994, eighty
  days after the eighth and a hundred and fifty-five after the seventh, in the open at the north end of a furnace ground
  with the ground looking on and a line already written in a fair hand about it. **Its cost, named once at ch 648: she
  cannot go back to not knowing.** Not the book and not the brother and not the name — knowing stops being a road back,
  and un-knowing is nobody's work. **Five hundred days lighter at the last page, the first day counted as the first,
  and not one of them going back. Not hungry. Nineteen years old.**
- **What she is at the last page.** A bar nine feet nine across her shoulders. A skin. A bag with a dish, a lump of
  clay, a ring a finger's width, a stub of a nib and a cake of ink she opened on the seventh of the month beginning
  1,874. **Two coppers she cannot count.** A book of eight leaves against her ribs: a date and a place on the first,
  four sentences on the second, `YUAN SHENG` on the third in her own hand, **and five that are hers and that are still
  blank.** **Four sheets of rice paper in a dead clerk's fair hand, folded four times, against her ribs on the side
  where the book is** — seven sentences, read alone at the fourth hour and then read out loud once in daylight in front
  of a boy of sixteen, and taken out of a locked drawer and not copied, because nobody writes one twice. Two things
  against her ribs and she has called neither of them anything.
- **What the batch changed.** She stood the ninth stage and lost the road back · she lived past the last ceiling and the
  layer never said another word · she broke her own sentence in a yard and walked west · she asked a man with a shut
  notebook whether staying is being kept and it is not · the last rate on that ground ended and the road behind it ·
  **Feng Lushi took a Lung at the second hour of the eleventh, wrote seven sentences in a fair hand, and died
  ordinary at about the fifth hour of the twenty-first of the month beginning 2,024** · she read the writing alone and
  could not put one word of it anywhere · **she read it out loud in daylight with a witness she had not asked for** ·
  **she said out loud, once, in a lit room, that she is staying, and told him she had been reading him as a column for
  eight years, and neither can be taken back** · she walked back east into the rows and named her brother in the open
  and it settled nothing, twice · **and at a turn at dawn with a bare board she said she is going up.**
- **The batch's three irreversible steps, and the outline names all three.** **ch 653 — the instrument is taken and spent
  in his hands and the drawer is shut for the rest of his life. ch 655 — Feng dies, ordinary, and it cannot be un-died.
  ch 658 — she stays knowing and it cannot be un-stayed.**
- **The fifth origin mouth is on the page at ch 652–654, in his fair hand, and it is the only one of the five written
  down.** A man of nineteen who has been nineteen for nineteen years and is the fourth piece of a thing with a name the
  clerk would not write · burned out at a fire in the east and what woke up was the piece that was not burned · the levy
  is one in twenty and it is not for the ground and it was made to find people like her · a girl who worked all of it
  out with a stick and two hands that hold his name and do not need her · and a thing seen truly and written truly is the
  only instrument in the world that can take a person through a wall.
- **Untouched and still open.** The window a hundred yards off the east side · the lime off the bar at ch 604 · the hut
  light · ***twenty-two days*, unplaced and equated to no dated interval** · the contents of the ash barrow · **forty-one
  strokes down the margin of the fourth sheet that Feng saw and would not word, and that she is not going to find
  out** · the five blank leaves.
- **Nothing is forgiven and nothing is thanked.** `thank` returns **0 across ch 646–660 and still does**, but

  remainder. There
  is no frame scene in this batch; the seventh stands at ch 644 and none follows it.
- **Who acted at the close.** She acted — an asking, a reading, a telling, a staying and a road up. He acted — the
  terms at the gate long ago, a frame scene, being read without stopping it, and answering every question she put in
  daylight. Feng acted — a Lung taken, a writing, a death. Wei Ran acted — a last rate and the road stopping. The man
  with the shut notebook acted — a shape and a refusal to describe the third time. A boy of sixteen acted — carried a
  bundle down four flights and did not look up for an hour and a half. A brother acted — nine years of silence about a
  fraction. A man of nine hundred walked past. **And the ground acted by needing nothing from her again, the fourth time
  in eleven volumes that it has done nothing at all.**

- **What the volume did.** She stood at the turn for eleven days with nine coppers she could not count and **asked
  the voice in her head what it was before it started counting, out loud, alone, in the dark, at ch 609, and it
  cannot be un-asked** · **the seventh stage cost her the ability to hear a telling, at ch 616** · **she wrote his
  hated name into a book no hand can reach at ch 624 and it cannot be put back un-written** · **the widow, the clerk
  and the kiln boy each gave her one morning of it** · **the eighth stage cost her the keeping, at ch 632** ·
  **he found out at ch 644, in the seventh frame scene, that a fragment cannot be used up by being known, and
  stopped being an entry** · **she told him at ch 645 that the tax is designed, in front of thirteen people, and
  stayed** · **the ninth stage cost her the road back at ch 648 and the layer said nothing after it** · **Feng Lushi
  took a Lung at the second hour of the eleventh of the month beginning 2,024, wrote seven sentences in a fair hand,
  and died ordinary at about the fifth hour of the twenty-first** · **she read it alone at ch 656 and could not put
  one word of it anywhere, and read it out loud at ch 657 with a witness she had not asked for** · **she said at
  ch 658, out loud, once, standing, in daylight, in a lit room with a witness in it, that she is staying, and told
  him she had been reading him as a column for eight years** · **she walked back east into the rows at ch 659, named
  her brother in the open, and it settled nothing, twice** · **and at a turn at dawn with a bare board she said, once,
  out loud, standing, in daylight: *I am going up***
- **The six irreversible steps, all verified in fact and not only in the card.** **ch 609** — the asking, un-askable ·
  **ch 624** — `YUAN SHENG` written, no solvent and no hand · **ch 645** — the tax named as designed and she stays ·
  **ch 653** — the instrument taken and spent in the moment it is in his hands · **ch 655** — he dies, ordinary, and
  it cannot be un-died · **ch 658** — she stays knowing and it cannot be un-stayed.
- **The five origin mouths, and the fifth one.** **ch 608** a furnace-man's mate of forty-five who worked the same
  fire twenty years · **ch 622** a senior disciple's widow who kept the harvest book nineteen years and opened it once
  in four days · **ch 636** the clerk who wrote `Yuan Sheng` in it once in a fair hand · **ch 640** a kiln boy grown
  old who carried the ash out the morning after · **ch 652–654 Feng Lushi, in his own fair hand, on four sheets of
  rice paper, seven sentences — the only one of the five written down on the page.** A man of nineteen who has been
  nineteen for nineteen years and is the fourth piece of a thing with a name the clerk would not write · burned out
  at a fire in the east and what woke up was the piece that was not burned · the levy is one in twenty, counted in
  days, off the board before the board goes up, made to a figure by somebody who chose the figure, and **it is not
  for the ground** · a girl who has counted with him for eight years and was told one word · two hands hold his name
  and do not need her · the thing was made to find people like her · **and a thing seen truly and written truly is
  the only instrument in the world that can take a person through a wall, and nobody writes one twice.**
- **Who is where.** Feng Lushi is **dead**, three feet from the back wall of the tower at Lowwater, buried at the
  fourth hour of the twenty-second of the month beginning 2,024, **and not thanked**. **Ning Wu is alive** in the
  rows, unforgiving, nine years in a ground he walked into at ten; she said his name out loud in the open at ch 659
  and he told her he found out about the fraction at three and has said nothing to anybody in nine years, and **it
  settles nothing.** **Shuyin walked past her at nine feet on a flat four miles short of a wall, going her way, with
  a shut book, said the fifth sentence he has ever said to her and it was not about himself, and is alive, old,
  correct and not thanked — and is not named on the page anywhere in the volume.** **The Hunter's request is still
  pending**, he is on the page twice at nine feet with the notebook shut under his arm, she asked him both times, and
  he asked her nothing either time. **Wei Ran** carried her the four miles for six coppers, said out loud, and the
  bed at the mill is finished.
- **The three Severance days, re-derived and not carried: 1,842 · 1,917 · 1,997, at intervals of 70 · 75 · 80, and 155
  from the seventh to the ninth and 225 from Severance 6.** The month-day rule is `((day − 134) mod 30) + 1` and
  every month-day in the volume reproduces under it. The going-lighter at any day D is **D − 1,560**, anchored on
  the eighteenth of the month beginning 1,544 = day 1,561; **seventeen of the volume's statements of it verify and
  the one that was wrong has been repaired.**
- **Untouched and still open.** The window a hundred yards off the east side · the lime off the bar at ch 604 · the
  hut light · ***twenty-two days*, still unplaced and equated to no dated interval anywhere in the volume** · the
  contents of the ash barrow · **forty-one strokes down the margin of the fourth sheet that Feng saw and would not
  word, and that she has said out loud she is not going to find out** · the five blank leaves · **how far the rows
  sit past the wall, which ch 618 and ch 659 give two ways and the close did not choose between them.**
- **Nothing is forgiven and nothing is thanked by her.** **`grep -oi forgiv` returns 1 across ch 601–660, not 0, and the
  0 this line used to carry was wrong.** The single instance is ch 659 and the word is `forgive`: *there is not one
  thing she could say about it that he would forgive* — a denial, in which she says there is nothing she could say
  that he would let go of. **Two methods disagree and this manuscript has published both:** on the strict word boundary
  `\bforgiv\b` the volume reads 0, because no inflected form but `forgive` exists on the page; on the substring
  `grep -oi forgiv`, named at `state/continuity.md` §*the volume as a whole*, it reads **1**. **The substring reading
  is the one to carry, because it is the one that does not let a form hide behind a boundary.** `the year` likewise
  reads **2, not 0**: ch 618, *the reason was not duty and was not kindness and was not the year* — a direct naming
  inside an explicit denial — and ch 637, *has not read his book back since the year it was written*, an unrelated
  sense. **Both instances refuse it and neither forgives anything.** The permissions at ch 609 and ch 645 are permissions
  and no line calls either of them a forgiveness. **No figure of what is left of him is stated anywhere in the
  volume** — what the clerk wrote down is
  his age. **There are seven frame scenes in the manuscript and the seventh is at ch 644; there is no eighth.**
- **The ending has not been spent.** The Anchor is not placed · release is not shown · the Sisterhood is not touched ·
  nobody explains why a bounded world needs readers · **Feng's fair handwriting is on the page once and read once and
  is not called a founding document** · the moral answer is unpaid and **nothing proves the crews wrong** · the
  Sincerity of the Scribe is never adjudicated and `Scribe` is spent once, in his own frame, as the thing he came
  off.

  *The Road Up*, ch 661–720.**

- **Ning Yan — SEVERANCE 9 throughout the batch.** No stage taken, no stage named, no bracket spent. **Nine stages past a
  ceiling that no longer exists. Nineteen years old.** Four fingers and a thumb on the right hand, the index gone. Right
  arm open four days in five. Four counts of breath everywhere and seven on a rise and nine on eight flights. Eight of
  eleven holds. The small open place low behind her fourth rib. **501 days lighter at the first page and 517 at the
  twenty-fourth and 524 at the last, the figure at any day D being D − 1,560.** Not hungry.
- **What the batch did.** She walked west off the turn at ch 661 and found out that the sentence she had said at ch 660
  had no object in it · **she went up four miles and eight flights and found a boy of sixteen keeping a lamp that ought not
  to be burning** at ch 662 · **the carter's answer came out at ch 663, and it is the first time in eleven volumes that
  anybody has said out loud that a bundle goes somewhere** · **she put Feng's four sheets back in the locked drawer at
  ch 664, on purpose, and told the boy where they were and said three things at once and was corrected by a boy of
  sixteen** · **she asked at ch 665, one at a time, in daylight, standing: can a person be announced without being
  designated, and it cannot be un-asked** · **she stood with the asking for a day at ch 666 and did not ask twice** · **the
  first half of the answer came at ch 667 — an announcement is not made to be heard, it is made to arrive, and the second
  half is addressed to a body and neither of them has ever been in a room** · **the room at the top of the stair was
  empty at ch 668 and has been for nine years, and the bar is on the landing side, and the finding is that there is
  nothing to receive an announcement and no one to receive it and those are one fact** · **she looked at what she has at
  ch 669 and it is a stub of a nib, a cake of ink and a hand that cannot write a false line, and it is all in her coat and
  none of it is in the building** · **she walked back to the turn at ch 670 and the woman of sixty told her the twenty-fourth
  at ch 671 and said she had been the last name on that board** · **at ch 672 she worked out out loud in daylight that a
  board is the only thing anybody has ever paid her for and is also the only thing the levy comes off, and it is one
  object** · **at ch 673 she offered to read the board, and said the part about not being paid out loud before anybody
  offered it to her, and the man of forty-nine made her say the number** · **and at ch 674 she got on it.**
- **The batch's irreversible step, ch 674, and there is exactly one.** **Her name and thirty days of the month beginning
  2,084 went into a column on the turn's board at the ninth hour of the twenty-fourth of the month beginning 2,054, in
  chalk, in a county hand, written by a man of forty-nine who was doing his month's work. She is on a board. The fraction
  can now reach her and there is no solvent for chalk and no hand in this county that will take a line out of a slate.**
- **The dated, un-movable deadline, pinned and met.** **The board went up on the first of the month beginning 2,084 at the
  first hour and came back down, and eleven people went past the frame in an hour and ten minutes and not one of them
  looked at the grit. The next first is day 2,114, thirty days on.** She counted eight firsts out loud at the ninth hour of
  the first: 2,114 · 2,144 · 2,174 · 2,204 · 2,234 · 2,264 · 2,294, and the eighth of them is the one `outline/volume-12.md`
  pins at ch 717.
- **Who is where.** **The boy of sixteen is in the tower at Lowwater**, on the fourth floor, copying nine sheets a day
  with a lamp in a cage on the fourth landing that he has now kept for thirty-five days, with his own days in the column
  and not his name in it, and a locked drawer he has never had the key to and has never knocked on. **The carter of
  sixty-four came up the hill on his own time on the ninth of the month beginning 2,054 to hand a boy of sixteen the only
  sentence anybody in thirty-one years had wanted, and went away down the hill with not one thing for it.** **The woman of
  sixty is on the bench**, and she was the last name on that board, on the second of a month beginning 1,806, for four
  years, and has sat on that bench for nine years since the eighteenth of the month beginning 1,814. **The man with the
  board is forty-nine, does not live at this turn, and has written four columns a month for nineteen years.** **Feng is
  not on a page.** **Ning Wu, Shuyin, Xie Changyin, the Hunter, Ning Yan’s brother and Wei Ran are not on the page in this
  batch**, and `Ning Wu`, `Shuyin`, `Hunter`, `Xie Changyin` and `Feng Lushi` all read **0** on the page.
- **The batch's physical card, paid.** **Eight flights up a mountain at the sixteenth of the month beginning 2,054 and
  eight flights down, fifty minutes down, sitting down twice, on an arm that was open, with the breath at seven on the
  third landing and at nine on the top step.** She came down the mountain again on the thirtieth, four days before the
  first, because four days is what a mountain and a stair and a road take if nothing goes wrong, and there is nothing she
  can do about anything going wrong.

- **Ning Yan — IMMORTAL VESSEL 1, taken at ch 685, day 2,128, the fifteenth of the month beginning 2,114, at the fourth hour
  of the afternoon, AT THE NORTH END OF THE ROWS, IN THE OPEN, in daylight, with about two hundred people in the ground. No
  announcement. No bracket. Nineteen. Corrected at the review: three sentences in ch 686 and ch 689 had put it inside the
  Order's building in front of a hundred readers, and it was never there.**
- **Body:** a bar nine feet nine **with no strap on it — the strap parted at the second hour of the fifth of the month
  beginning 2,084, six miles east of the turn, and every lift and set-down has cost a count of twenty-two to fifty-four
  since, about four hundred times.** Right index gone, four fingers and a thumb. Right arm open four days in five, and it
  was open when she wrote the line on the board. Four counts of breath with seven on a rise. Eight of eleven holds. The
  small open place low behind her fourth rib, the size of a coin. **Five hundred and eighty days lighter, not one of them
  gone back. Not hungry.**
- **Against her ribs: one object.** The book of eight leaves — a date and a place on the first, four sentences on the
  second, `YUAN SHENG` on the third in four capital strokes in her own hand, five that are hers and are blank. **She has
  called it nothing and Batch 0002 did not name it for her.** **The four sheets of a dead clerk's fair hand are in a locked
  drawer four miles west of a square and the key is in the front of her bag and she did not use it in this batch.**
- **In her hand:** two coppers she cannot count. In her bag: a dish, a lump of clay, a ring a finger’s width, a stub of a
  nib, a cake of ink opened on the seventh of the month beginning 1,874 and never let dry.
- **What the fifteen chapters changed.** **She went east twenty miles and found a building standing in the open at the end of
  a made road with forty people in front of it · a man with a book looked up and said her name, which is the first time in
  eleven volumes she has been recognised by a thing that keeps records · she was read a sheet a second time in her life and
  refused it a second time with a figure in her hand · and she worked out that she could never have been recruited at all,
  because confirming a reading means being able to read it and there is no hand in that building that can read hers ·
  she stayed six days in a room she cannot be paid in and cannot be given anything in and read the sheets nobody else there
  can read, for nothing, with no line anywhere that says she was there · she found a keeper’s sheet nailed to a wall at
  eye height, ninety-eight thousand against a sum of ninety-five thousand and eighty, and the difference of two thousand nine
  hundred and twenty is on nobody’s sheet and no hand in the building owns it · she said the total out loud in daylight in
  a room and a hundred people did arithmetic for ten minutes · she took the stage after the ninth at one hundred and
  thirty-one days and not seventy and named its cost: she cannot be given a shape · she wrote one, on a board, in her own
  hand, with four fingers and a thumb, on an arm that was open · a cart stopped at that board for the first time in nine
  years and a carter who cannot read it said her brother’s name — his name — twice and did not know he had said it ·
  the bill for that was raised out loud at the tenth hour of a night and answered by nobody · and she agreed, standing, in
  daylight, in front of two hundred people, to be recorded while it happens, with six terms and no seventh.**
- **The batch’s irreversible step:** **ch 689 — she lets him keep the record, out loud, standing, in daylight, with the terms
  in it, after saying no first and then saying why she was changing it. A document agreed to in daylight is not a document
  that can be taken back, and it is also not a document that can be added to.**

2, taken at ch 702, day 2,206**, and the stage named itself in the ordinary prose with no bracket and no panel. Four fingers and a
thumb, a bar nine feet nine with no strap on it, an arm open four days in five, four counts of breath with seven on a rise,
**six hundred and eighty days lighter and not one of them gone back, not hungry.** Against her ribs the same one object; the four
sheets are still in a locked drawer and the five blank leaves are still blank.

**WHAT THE FIFTEEN CHAPTERS DID, in order.** She lived three days inside a record that is being kept of her out loud and worked
out that a record said out loud is a thing she cannot check against a page · the third first went by and she was not at the frame,
and a **nine-inch strip of blank paper came east out of the machinery above the line and there is no form on it she can be given**
· **the eighth frame scene at ch 694**, and the finding in it is that a shape is a thing that has to be kept by somebody, and he
has decided to ask her to keep one and **cannot say the day** · she worked her own total out over six days on paper she made by
hand out of rags and **said it out loud standing in daylight at ch 697: four days and one hour and twelve minutes, eighty-one lines
and the eighty-first was the day she said it on** — that is Batch 0003's irreversible step and it cannot be un-said · seven days
inside a number she has said out loud, in which she answers her own ch 665 asking by accident, out loud: the answer is no, and the
question was wrong · the fourth first went up in front of her at the frame, and on the board she saw, for the first time in her
life, what a name in a column is worth — **a fifth of a day of the days that name had in it, every name, every month** — and the
figure under every one of them · she went west, out through the gate and across two miles of flat with nothing to lie down on, to the two posts, where the
slate has been wiped and put back with her name on it and nothing has been taken off it that she can see · **Immortal Vessel 2 at
ch 702, seventy-eight days and not seventy, and its cost
named once — she cannot be met where she is expected** · the cost carried at scale, a stage that nobody could come and find ·
**ch 704, the hardest chapter in the volume: a number she can work out is a number two hundred people on a row can work out, and
she does not go and tell them and she does not stop counting, and it is not resolved** · and **ch 705, the sixth first going past
at a wall with the seventh twenty-four days off.**

**WHAT THIS BLOCK IS NOT.** It is not a review. The batch has been through no review of its own; `state/continuity.md` carries no

with the shut notebook and was told that a record belongs to whoever keeps it and that a line does not come back** · **she worked out that a line has to be true, findable and not her, and that the said nothing and is not going to** · **the seventh first went by at the frame with the slate turned over and the figure under her name one hundred and eighty-eight days and she wrote nothing** · **she ends of a row of stones** · **she went up the mountain for the last time, stood at the outside of a low door and did not go through it, and an old man with a shut book went past her on the mountain **Day 2,300, the seventh of the month beginning 2,294, at the third hour: she is standing on the first step of a second stair inside a room at the top of eight flights and eight hundred and eighty steps in a tower four miles up a mountain from a square, at IMMORTAL VESSEL 3, nineteen years old, seven hundred and forty days lighter with none going back, not hungry, with three fingers and a thumb on the right hand and an arm that has been open since the morning she wrote the line and is not going to close.** Four counts of breath, seven on a rise, nine standing still at the top of eight flights. The small open place low behind her fourth rib, the size of a coin. Eight of eleven holds. Against her ribs one object: a book of eight leaves with a date and a place on the first, four sentences on the second, `YUAN SHENG` on the third, and five blank that are hers and stay blank. In a bag at the bottom of the stair: a dish, a lump of clay, a ring the width of a finger, a stub of a nib, a cake of ink opened on the seventh of the month beginning 1,874, a key to a locked drawer, and a nine-inch strip with eighty-one lines. In a book above her: nineteen words in her own hand with a date and a place, true, with ten days, twenty-one hours and thirty-six minutes in them. **Yuan: Court since ch 687, present tense, no figure of what is left of him, the bill settled at ch 718 on listed work out of the ground under her feet.** The low door stands open; the long table, the lamp in a cage burning at the far end, the second stair out of the far side. **She went up one step and stopped there. That is the last thing in the volume.**

## Volume 13 planned — `outline/volume-13.md` exists and what the plan fixed

**VOLUME 13, *The Immortal Court*, ch 721–780, IS PLANNED. The planning phase wrote `outline/volume-13.md` and `workspace/volume-13-batch-0001/PROMPT.md` and nothing else — no second batch directory, no `.done` marker, and no chapter prose. The one and only next phase is `workspace/volume-13-batch-0001/PROMPT.md`, VOLUME 13 BATCH 0001, ch 721–735, days 2,301–2,360, pressure *the bundles*.** Goal, chapter range and title are fixed by `outline/series.md` §9 and none of the three is re-planned: *In this volume, Ning Yan must survive being small in a world of enormous people, or the Court decides what she is for her.* Record in `state/continuity.md` under *VOLUME 13 PLANNING*. **The bar-to-carry correction made above to this file is the only in-place correction this phase made; everything else it added is appended here.**

**THE POSITION DOES NOT MOVE. She is one step up a second stair at day 2,300 and Batch 0001 opens on day 2,301, the eighth of the month beginning 2,294, and the first page of Volume 13 is the last page of Volume 12 with one more day on it.**

### What the plan fixed, and a Volume 13 drafter reads it before any card

1. **Central pressure: she is in a room where things are decided by a hand, and the hand belongs to somebody else, and she is the smallest thing in it.** The Court is the receiving end of the announcement layer — Feng Lushi's job with the ceiling taken off — and **deciding takes one look, there is no second look and there is no appeal.** Nobody in the room is a villain; they are correct, brief and bored, which is `outline/series.md` §6's line for Volumes 13–14.
2. **The lamp is the instrument, the clock, the currency and the cost.** It is not an oil lamp; there is no oil in the building and there has not been for nine years, and it is fed by hand, and **she measures the lamp and the person at the table and works out that they are the same substance.** She does not learn what it is and she does not learn what it is paying for.
3. **The figure at the head of her row is her own total — ten days, twenty-one hours and thirty-six minutes — in her own hand**, the line she wrote on a slate at the two posts at ch 717 and carried up that stair at ch 718. There is no stage on it and there is no name on it, **and the figure is still growing at one hour and twelve minutes a day, and above the line nobody loses a day, so there is nobody above the line who could ever know that the row is going stale.** That is the volume's thesis in one figure, and it answers the opening question on the page: the room knows what she is worth to the hour and it does not know her.
4. **Immortal Vessel 4 · 5 · 6 at days 2,403 · 2,461 · 2,524**, at intervals of **113 · 58 · 63**, at ch 746 · 761 · 776, with costs named once each: **she cannot be given a thing · she cannot be measured with a person · she cannot be looked at again.** `Sisterhood` is 0 and no person is bound in Volume 13.
5. **Yuan's second rung of Court is readability, taken at ch 748 and not before**, when she says `YUAN Sheng` out loud in the room. **Raised and not paid — nothing speaks to him, and `outline/ending.md` §1 and §2 keep the address and the archive for Volume 15.** The **ninth frame scene is at ch 758** and it is about a place at the table with his shape on it that he does not get into. **And this does not move `outline/ending.md` §1's date: §1 dates his being readable *by the Scribe* to Volume 14, and what ch 748 makes readable is the room's hand, which is the rung of Court and not the Scribe's finding.**
6. **One change to the oldest number in the manuscript.** At ch 735 she puts a day into the lamp on day 2,357 and it does not come back, and **from that day the figure she is lighter by is `D − 1,559` and not `D − 1,560`, permanently**: 741 at 2,301 · 796 at 2,356, the last figure of the old series · 797 at 2,357 before the lamp · **798 at 2,357 after it, two days in one day** · 801 at 2,360 · **981 at day 2,540.**
7. **Four batches**, none running another's pressure: **0001 the bundles** (ch 721–735, 2,301–2,360) · **0002 the row** (736–750, 2,361–2,420) · **0003 the measure** (751–765, 2,421–2,480) · **0004 the second look** (766–780, 2,481–2,540). Eight firsts inside the volume at **2,324 · 2,354 · 2,384 · 2,414 · 2,444 · 2,474 · 2,504 · 2,534**, two in every batch, and **no first of a month is a stage day.**
8. **The bracket budget is zero in sixty, argued on its own grounds:** above the line the layer is not in the room, and the room is what the layer is for.
9. **Inherited and not re-derived:** the whole of *VOLUME 12 CLOSE* and *VOLUME 12 CLOSE — REVIEW REPAIR*, including the stale county total *thirteen days* (a Volume 12 inheritance, not Volume 13's to settle), the four accounts with no fifth, the unattributed 2,920, the 301 of 912, the open rows-past-the-wall question, and **the bar dated nowhere and taken up when she was nine — Volume 13 may not give it a day, a month-begin or a year-count.**

**What Volume 13 may not preview: the moral answer. `outline/ending.md` §1's Anchor, the bargain, the address and the four hundred bound people are all untouched, and `Ascendant` and `Choir` are 0 on the page. `Court` may be spoken at most once, in Batch 0004, as the word for a building.**

**Day 2,360, the seventh of the month beginning 2,354, at the ninth hour: she is sitting on the floor of the room at the top of eight flights and eight hundred and eighty steps in a tower four miles up a mountain from a square, with her back against the doorframe and her right arm open across her knees, at IMMORTAL VESSEL 3, nineteen years old, not hungry, with three fingers and a thumb on the right hand, four counts of breath and seven on a rise and nine standing still, the small open place low behind her fourth rib the size of a coin, eight of eleven holds, and a lamp at the far end of a long table that is higher than it has ever been.**

**THE FIGURE SHE IS LIGHTER BY HAS CHANGED AND STAYS CHANGED. It was `D − 1,560` and it was correct at every day up to and including day 2,356, which was the last figure of the old series, seven hundred and ninety-six. At day 2,357 she put a day into the lamp in the cage and the step happened inside that one day: seven hundred and ninety-seven before, seven hundred and ninety-eight after, two days in one day. From that day the figure at any day D is `D − 1,559` and it is that for the rest of the manuscript. 799 at 2,358 · 800 at 2,359 · 801 at 2,360 · 981 at day 2,540. The 980 that the old series would have given at 2,540 is withdrawn.**

**And the mark moved with it.** The light comes down two of her own hand a day against a line cut into the stone of the wall fifty-eight of her own hand below the top of the cage, which is twenty-nine days. The mark from the turn on 2,354 was day 2,383 and the day she spent moves it to **2,384, the first of the month beginning 2,384, which is the day the next bundle comes up that stair.** That is the first day of slack in a month that has never once been spent in four hundred years, and she spent it, with nobody asked for it, and it does not come back.

- **The room.** A long table down the middle of it running away from the door, twenty-two paces long, four feet off the floor, with a lamp in a cage on a shelf at the far end and a stub of candle in a dish with one grey bloom of cold wax in it and no wick. **One person at the table, no name, both hands on the near edge, who has not moved once in sixty days and has not looked up and has not spoken.** A bundle of papers on the floor under the far end, tied with a cord round twice and not knotted, which she has had her arm into and has not pulled. Forty boards in a stack under the middle of the table, face up, in the dark. **A strip of pale wood under the near end, flat on the stone, face up, a foot long and two inches across, with a head and a column and one figure in the column: ten days, twenty-one hours and thirty-six minutes, in her own hand, and no name and no place and no date and nothing about what she is.** **And a bar nine feet nine flat against the wall on the left, broken end towards the door, left there on purpose at ch 731, and it is there now.**
- **The stair.** The second stair goes up out of the far side of the room and comes back down to the floor of the same room out of a place in the wall behind the lamp's shelf. She has counted it to **nine hundred** and lost the count there and put the number nowhere. **The whole is nine hundred and four and she does not have that figure until ch 754.** The tower's own stair is eight hundred and eighty in two runs of four hundred and forty and the two are not added.
- **What she can reach.** The table, on the fourth try, on day 2,316, and she can get up on it and walk its top on her hands and knees. She chose not to put a hand on it once in the forty minutes of a reading with boards on it.
- **What she has not got.** The nib, the ink, the dish, the clay, the ring, the key to the locked drawer and the nine-inch strip of eighty-one lines, all in a bag at the bottom of the first four flights. **She decided at ch 734 that she is not going down for them and has not gone down.**
- **The five blank leaves against her ribs stay blank.** Nothing from this room has gone into them and she said so out loud at ch 721, standing, in daylight, and did not improve on it. **The bar is dated nowhere and she took it up when she was nine.**
- **The six terms stand and there is no seventh and none was asked for in this batch. The lever was carried zero times. The fifth term was not spent.** The asking at ch 665 is untouched and its answer is not re-voiced.
- **Yuan: no frame scene in this batch, and the ninth is at ch 758.** He speaks in the present tense three times, at ch 727, ch 733 and ch 735. He is addressable and nothing more, **his name is still on the third leaf of a book against her ribs and nowhere else, he is not in a record, he is not readable, and no figure of what is left of him is on any page.** At ch 735 she says to him the first thing she has said to anybody that is a plan.
- **No stage is named in ch 721–735. Immortal Vessel 3 stands from ch 716 on day 2,290. Immortal Vessel 4 is at ch 746, day 2,403, at one hundred and thirteen days.** No first of a month in Volume 13 is a stage day.
- **Firsts in Volume 13 so far: 2,324 and 2,354 taken. Six remain: 2,384 · 2,414 · 2,444 · 2,474 · 2,504 · 2,534.**
- **The board at the north end of the rows: `(2,360 − 2,128) × 4 = 928` and she cannot see it. The accruing total on the slate is 281 days at day 2,357 and is on no page.** The staleness of the row is `(D − 2,294) × 72 minutes` and is three days, three hours and thirty-six minutes at day 2,357 and is twelve days, seven hours and twelve minutes at day 2,540, and **there is not one person above the line who could ever know that, because there is not one person above this line who loses a day.**
