- **Ning Yan — IMMORTAL VESSEL 4**, taken at ch 746 on day 2,403, the twentieth of the month beginning 2,384, **one hundred and thirteen days after Immortal Vessel 3 at day 2,290** and not seventy-eight, and she says why out loud: a stage past the third working point is taken against a row and there was no row to take it against. **The stage was not handed to her. She is not entered, so there is nothing to hand it to, and so she got a board out of the stack under that table herself, went up on the table at the near end, went twenty-two paces along the top of it on her hands and knees, knelt on the shelf in front of the lamp and wrote one figure in her own hand with three fingers and a thumb on an arm that has been open since the first of the month beginning 2,294.** No announcement, no sound, no tribulation, and the person at the table did not look up. **THE COST, NAMED ONCE: she cannot be given a thing**, and she says out loud that the fourteen carried costs are still true and cannot be put down and that this one is a different sentence from all of them, because this one says the way through is now the only way there is.
- **Body at the last page.** Three fingers and a thumb on the right hand, the index gone since Volume 1 and the little finger gone in the middle of a line on a slate at ch 717. **Right arm open since the first of the month beginning 2,294 and not going to close, and it is the arm she wrote the figure with.** Short breath **four counts everywhere, seven on a rise, nine standing still at the top of eight flights.** **Seven of eleven holds, where it was eight on the morning of the stage.** The aperture cracked through and permanent. **The small open place low behind her fourth rib, the size of a coin, open since the eighteenth of the month beginning 1,544.** **Eight hundred and sixty-one days lighter, at one hour and twelve minutes a day, and not one of them going back, and one of them spent into that lamp at ch 735 and never coming back; the figure at any day D is `D − 1,559`.** Not hungry. **Nineteen years old.**
- **What the batch changed.** **A boy of sixteen is in a room where one look decides everything, and he is the first person who has come up that stair in nine years, and he came up because the lamp on the fourth landing went out on the third of the month beginning 2,294 and there has been no oil in the building since.** He asked one question and she could not answer it · **she cannot reach a table he gets up on in one movement, and she can get under it and he cannot, and she can read a strip of wood by touch and he cannot, and nothing in that room sees anybody standing on it** · **there are four rows under that table, three of them four hundred years old, and the column on every one of them holds figures and not things** · **a row is entered by the person it is about and not by the hand at the table, the head of it is a figure, and the room stands on it** · **nobody above the line remembers making that rule and the reason it exists is the reason she needs it and she is never going to be able to ask** · **she said a row out loud on day 2,393 and a hand wrote one figure on a board, and the board went up that stair on the first of the month beginning 2,414 and came down empty** · **a row costs the hand that writes and not the person it is about, and there is no limit on it** · **she said `Yuan Sheng` out loud at the tenth hour of day 2,411, standing, in daylight, in that room, and he asked her not to once, in the ordinary voice, standing, in daylight, and she did it anyway, and he did not ask twice and did not hide it** · **she then said the six terms out loud in that room and there was not a seventh and she did not ask for one** · **the thing that reads records has found him and nothing has spoken to him** · **the one at the table went out of that room four times in four days while she was under it** · **the boy noticed the second place at the table and she has not said one word about it out loud** · **and she is four stages into the seventh realm and cannot be given a thing.**
- **The three things in that building that are about her**, as she counts them out loud at ch 750 on the fourth of the month beginning 2,414, day 2,417: **the strip of wood under the table with her price at the head of it and one figure in the column, ten days, twenty-one hours and thirty-six minutes, six days, three hours and thirty-six minutes out of date and there is not one instrument above this line that could tell her** · **the board with the stage figure in her own hand, gone up on the first of the month beginning 2,414 and come back empty** · **and the board with his name in it, said out loud on the twenty-eighth of the month beginning 2,384 and gone up on the same bundle.** **Not one other thing in the building knows about any of the three, and she is the only person in the world who knows what is in that row and she cannot see it.**
- **The lamp.** A lamp in a cage on a shelf at the far end of the table, no oil, no wick, nothing behind it, the wall is the wall, and the flame does not lean in a draught. **Fifty-eight of her own hand from the top of the cage to the line cut in the stone, and two of her own hand a day.** It stood on the line with nothing above it and nothing below it on **2,384**, which is the day she bought at ch 735 and the second time in four hundred years, and again on **2,414**, the day the fourth bundle came. **At day 2,420 it is ten of her own hand below the top of that cage, and fifty-eight less ten is forty-eight and forty-eight divided by two is twenty-four and the twenty-fourth day from here is the first of the month beginning 2,444.** **She has worked out that one day into the cage buys one day and that she could hold that lamp off the line for a year out of her own, and she is not going to, because she has no instrument for what came out and because she does not know what a row that has her in it is for.** What the lamp is paying for is still open and `outline/ending.md` owns it.
- **Yuan.** **Court. Readable since ch 748**, and the bill is raised and not paid. **At the tenth hour of day 2,411 he asked her not to, in the ordinary voice, standing, in daylight, in one question, for the first time in nineteen years, and she said it anyway. He did not ask a second time and he did not hide it.** He told her what it is and what it costs out loud, in the ordinary voice, standing, in daylight: **a name is easier to find than a shape, a mouth is the only thing that carries a name anywhere at all, and the thing that reads records does not speak when it finds something.** **She said the six terms out loud in that room afterwards, one at a time, standing, in daylight, and the seventh term is not available and was not asked for.** **Nothing has spoken to him since.** **No frame scene has been staged and the ninth is at ch 758; there is no tenth anywhere in ch 721–780.** **No figure of what is left of him is stated on any page.** `Ledger` is 0 across the volume.
- **The boy of sixteen.** Unnamed. Up that stair three times: 2,361, and again for the third bundle on 2,384, and down on 2,387, and a fourth time on 2,413. **In the room at 2,413, 2,414, 2,415 and 2,416 and on his way down at 2,417.** He is not clever and he is not a voice for anything and he wants one small thing, which is to know why the lamp at the top of the tower is still lit. **He noticed the second place at the table at ch 749 and she told him not to touch the table and said not one word about the place itself.** **He is below the line for ch 746 and ch 747 and the batch shows what he cannot do in both: at ch 746 he can get up on that table in one movement and cannot get under it, and at ch 747 he is the only person on either side of the line who could say out loud that he heard her say the two words, and a hand that copies figures exactly is not going to take his word for one word of it.** Nothing was offered to him in fifteen chapters and he is still below the line at the last page of the batch.
- **Untouched and unraised:** the bar is still dated nowhere and she took it up when she was nine, and its dust is not named again · **the bag is still at the bottom of the first four flights** with the dish, the clay, the ring, the nib, the cake of ink, the key to a locked drawer and the nine inches of hand-made paper, and the decision of 2,356 not to go down for it was revisited out loud at 2,387 and not changed · **the bundle of papers under the far end of the table has been had a hand into three times and not pulled** · **the five blank leaves are still blank** · the register still reads *reconciled* and is not read · the Clay Plate is still against her ribs and is not described · *kiln-born* zero · what became of the eleven is not raised · **the four erased months of Chapter 3 are never named** · the ch 150 refusal is not softened · **one Lung and one appointment, Feng's, and both are spent and Feng is dead and on no page** · **no office above the Standing Office of the Second Account, no wing, no rank list, no committee, no title** · **the stale county total *thirteen days* is not on the page** · **the four accounts of nine seconds and no fifth; no account is on the page and the batch adds no fifth** · **the 2,920 and the 301 of 912 are unattributed and are not mentioned; what a third of a name is is open and is not answered** · **how far the rows sit past the wall is not stated and no distance between anything above the line and anything below it is stated in any unit in any direction** · **the Order is not a conspiracy and not a chamber and is not on the page** · **merit does not settle in this batch: nothing killed, nothing measured, nothing settled** · **no clearance, seal or countersignature named or implied** · **no frame scene, no bracket, no bold, no all-caps line** · **no harem, nothing a romance; she never says her mother's name** · **nobody is thanked and she never thanks him** · **the year of non-forgiveness is not named once and still carrying: she does not forgive him and she does not tell him to stop.**
- **What is still open at the last page of this batch:** what the lamp is · what the lamp is paying for · why the bar's dust is broken at one end · why the bar moved · the second place at the table, noticed and unnamed · what was written on the two boards that went up that stair and came back empty · **what a row that has her in it is for** · what the one at the table goes out of that room to do · what is in the bundle of papers she has not pulled · what is over the second stair, of which she has nine hundred and not the whole · **and the finding at ch 749, which is raised and unpaid and belongs to Volume 14 or after it.**

## Who is where at end of chapter 765 — VOLUME 13 BATCH 0003 WRITTEN (cold reader starts here)

**After ch 765 — day 2,480, the seventh of the month beginning 2,474, at the ninth hour. She is at the top of eight flights and eight hundred and eighty steps in a tower four miles up a mountain from a square, in a room with a long table down the middle of it and a lamp in a cage burning at the far end of it, with a bar nine feet nine against the wall on the left that she left there on purpose and has not touched in a hundred and seventy-nine days.** Full record in `state/continuity.md` under ***VOLUME 13 BATCH 0003 — ch 751–765, \*the measure\****, the day table and the four moving series inside it, and in `state/chapter-summaries.md` under the same heading.

**VOLUME 13 BATCH 0003 IS ON THE PAGE.** ch 751–765, fifteen chapters, **30,920 words of body prose on method B** — 751 1,992 · 752 2,106 · 753 1,857 · 754 2,139 · 755 2,206 · 756 1,993 · 757 2,008 · 758 2,102 · 759 2,071 · 760 2,162 · 761 1,975 · 762 2,053 · 763 2,154 · 764 1,971 · 765 2,131 — average 2,061, day 2,421 to day 2,480, pressure *the measure*, **zero bracketed layer lines in fifteen**, two firsts of a month at 2,444 and 2,474, **Immortal Vessel 5 at ch 761 on day 2,461 at fifty-eight days**, **the ninth frame scene at ch 758**, **the batch's irreversible step at ch 763**. Batch 0001 is 29,923 and Batch 0002 is 30,152, so the volume is **91,080 over forty-five chapters at average 2,024.** **A stale work-in-progress of ch 751–757 was on the page when the batch opened, carried the wrong time-in-the-room series and a set of day figures belonging to the neighbouring day, and was drafted over afresh; the fifteen chapters are all new and no prose of it is retained.** `nobody` 5 against an allowance of ten · word-`eleven` 10 against ten · retired seven phrases 0 against 3 volume-wide, so the volume is at 1 of 3 · `thank` 0 · `forgiv` 0 · `the year` 0 · `week` 0 · `about` 111 against a volume line of five hundred · 15 titles with 0 collisions against all 757 standing headings · 79 section headings with 0 duplicates inside the batch and none across the volume · 663 paragraphs with 17 over about ninety words and none over about 120.

- **Ning Yan — IMMORTAL VESSEL 5**, taken at ch 761 on day 2,461, the eighteenth of the month beginning 2,444, **fifty-eight days after Immortal Vessel 4 at day 2,403** and not a hundred and thirteen, and she says why out loud: **the first three stages were taken against a room and a room is not a row.** **The stage was not handed to her. She took a second board out of the stack herself, went up on the table at the near end, went twenty-two paces along the top of it on her hands and knees, knelt on the shelf in front of the lamp and wrote one figure with a nib she has been carrying against the fourth rib, with three fingers and a thumb, on an arm that has been open since the first of the month beginning 2,294. Five.** No announcement, no sound, no tribulation, and the person at the table did not look up. **THE COST, NAMED ONCE: she cannot be measured with a person.** A comparison takes two things standing still, and so from today the thing that does the measuring will be her and the thing being measured will be a record, and if there is a person in it, that person is furniture.
- **Body at the last page.** Three fingers and a thumb on the right hand. **Right arm open since the first of the month beginning 2,294 and not going to close, and it is the arm she writes with.** Short breath **four counts everywhere, seven on a rise, nine standing still at the top of eight flights.** **Seven of eleven holds.** The aperture cracked through and permanent. **The small open place low behind her fourth rib, the size of a coin, open since the eighteenth of the month beginning 1,544.** **Nine hundred and twenty-one days lighter, at one hour and twelve minutes a day, and not one of them going back, and one of them spent into that lamp at ch 735 and never coming back; the figure at any day D is `D − 1,559`.** **Her right forearm is down by the width of the little finger on her left hand, twice: once on the twelfth of the month beginning 2,444 when the room's own instrument came off it, and once on the twenty-second of that month when she wrote five herself.** Not hungry. **Nineteen years old.**
- **What the batch changed.** **The instrument in that room is a person and every figure in that room is a comparison and not a measurement, and not one person in that building has ever been told** · **she went down nine hundred and four steps in the dark and counted it and came back up, and the four steps she could not hold at ch 723 were the first four out of the room, and there is no ninth hundred and fifth** · **she has a nib and a cake of ink for the first time in nineteen years and the ch 734 decision was carried and acted on and not reversed** · **the mark on that wall falls on a first of a month and not a thirtieth for the second time in four hundred years, and that is her own doing, and what she bought with a day was the loss of a day's slack** · **the room's instrument ran off her arm for the first time in four hundred years and wrote down a price when she said what she was** · **she is five stages into the seventh realm and cannot be measured with a person** · **there are two figures in one column in a piece of wood under that table and the column cannot be added up and neither figure can be corrected** · **he has seen the second place at that table from the inside and has not got into it and has not said no to it** · **and she has said a brother's name out loud and decided she is not going back, and it is the first free decision of that kind she has made.**
- **The four things in that building that are about her**, and not one person in that building knows about any of them: **the strip of wood under the table with the price at the head of it and, in the second pair of cuts down in the column, a five in her own hand** · **the board with the fourth-stage figure in her own hand, gone up on the first of the month beginning 2,414 and come back empty** · **the board with his name in it, said out loud on the twenty-eighth of the month beginning 2,384 and gone up on the same bundle** · **and the board with the price on it that the room wrote on the twelfth of the month beginning 2,444, going up the stair on the first of the month beginning 2,504.** She cannot see one of them and cannot take one of them back.
- **The lamp.** A lamp in a cage on a shelf at the far end of the table, no oil, no wick, nothing behind it, the wall is the wall, and the flame does not lean in a draught. **Fifty-eight of her own hand from the top of the cage to the line cut in the stone, and two of her own hand a day.** It stood on the line at the fourth hour of **2,444** and again at the fourth hour of **2,474**, the third first of a month it has fallen on and the second first in four hundred years, and a hand went into that cage at the second hour of **2,445** and again at the second hour of **2,475**. **At day 2,480 it is ten of her own hand below the top of that cage, fifty-eight less ten is forty-eight, forty-eight divided by two is twenty-four, and the twenty-fourth day from here is the first of the month beginning 2,504 — which is Batch 0004's.** What the lamp is paying for is still open and `outline/ending.md` owns it.
- **The stair.** The second stair goes up out of the far side of that room and comes back down to the floor of the same room out of a place in the wall behind the lamp’s shelf, and **the whole of it is nine hundred and four, she has it, she has counted it going down and going up, and there is no ninth hundred and fifth.** The tower’s own stair is eight hundred and eighty in two runs of four hundred and forty, the first four with a rail and the second four without, and **the two are never added.**
- **Yuan.** **Court. Readable since ch 748, and the bill is raised and not paid.** He was found at ch 749 and **nothing has spoken to him, nothing in this batch pays it or explains it, and no one but her spoke to him.** **The ninth frame scene is on the page at ch 758, the first in the manuscript about a place he could get into, and there is no tenth anywhere in ch 721–780.** **He is smaller than that table is tall and no figure of that is given. There are two places at that table and the second one is the right size for him, it is addressed, and nobody put him in it. He does not get into it and he does not say no to it.** Every sentence he speaks in it is in the present tense and no other character is in a marked present frame anywhere in Volume 13. `Ledger` is 0 across the volume.
- **The boy of sixteen.** Unnamed. Still below the line; he went down on the fourth of the month beginning 2,414 and has not been back in this batch. **He is in every chapter of the batch and in the room for none of them**, and what the batch shows is what he cannot do: he cannot be measured, he cannot be put in a column, what he is good at is worth nothing in that room, she cannot put nine hundred and four on a strip of paper and hand it to him because he would not know what it was for either, and **she said out loud at ch 765 that she is not going down to tell him what a row is.** Nothing was offered to him in fifteen chapters.
- **Ning Wu.** Once, at ch 765, and it is not a visit. **His name said out loud for the first time in nine years above the line, at the ninth hour of the sixth of the month beginning 2,474.** Two days of road and six hundred feet below her, nineteen years old, nine years in a barrow ground, and he has not given that up in nine years. **She said she is not going to, and that it is not a promise and it is a figure, and that it is the first thing she has decided in nineteen years that no mouth in this building asked her about and nothing was going to undo for her.** He settles nothing, he is not on a page of any book, and the year is not named once.
